Wink Reyes Valley

A family Minecraft server

Survival with RPG skills, cash for everyday play, and big adventures to share. Play together on a phone, iPad, tablet, PC, Mac or console.

Add our server in Minecraft

On a phone, tablet, or Windows PC with Minecraft installed, this opens the game and may offer to add our server with the Bedrock port. If it doesn't fill anything in, it hasn't worked; use the steps below instead, they always work. On console or Java this button won't do anything.

wrv.duckdns.org

Also works: winkreyesvalley.duckdns.org or winkreyes.duckdns.org (same server, same ports)

Two ports, one address. Bedrock (phone, iPad, tablet, Windows, console): wrv.duckdns.org port 25622. Java (PC or Mac): wrv.duckdns.org:20795.

Both Bedrock ports work: since about 7:09 PM Central on October 26, 2026, Bedrock (phones, tablets, Windows and consoles) joins on port 25622 (the new way) or the old port 20795 (the old way), so you can keep the port that works on your device. Bedrock 26.60, planned for October 27, drops the old connection method on devices after they install that client update, so use 25622 when you can. Keep your saved address; wrv.duckdns.org and the older addresses all work. Consoles that join through BedrockConnect can use either port too.

How to join

Phone, iPad or tablet

The Add our server in Minecraft button above may open Minecraft for you. If it doesn't add our server, do it yourself with these steps:

  1. Play, then Servers, then Add Server
  2. Name: Wink Reyes Valley
  3. Address: wrv.duckdns.org
  4. Port: 25622 (the Bedrock port)
  5. The first time you join, tap Trust on the pop-up

Windows (Bedrock)

  1. Play, then Servers, then Add Server
  2. Name: Wink Reyes Valley
  3. Address: wrv.duckdns.org
  4. Port: 25622 (the Bedrock port)
  5. The first time you join, tap Trust on the pop-up

Java on PC or Mac

  1. Multiplayer, then Add Server
  2. Address: wrv.duckdns.org:20795

Java keeps port 20795. Nothing changed for Java players.

Xbox, PlayStation, Switch

  1. In your console network settings, set primary DNS to 104.238.130.180 and secondary to 8.8.8.8 (this is the public BedrockConnect helper, not our server)
  2. Restart Minecraft, then go to Servers
  3. Join one of the featured servers. Look for Mineville or Lifeboat first; The Hive, Galaxite or Enchanted also work. The list on your screen may look different or be in a different order; any of those five opens the same menu. CubeCraft does not work for this
  4. The BedrockConnect menu opens. Pick Connect to a Server
  5. Enter wrv.duckdns.org with Bedrock port 25622 or 20795. Keep a saved entry that already works; you do not need to remove it just because it uses 20795.

Both ports work: since about 7:09 PM Central on October 26, 2026, consoles can use port 25622 or the old port 20795 in BedrockConnect, with the address wrv.duckdns.org (the older addresses also work). Port 25622 is the one to pick for the long run.

Heads up for consoles: after the Bedrock update on October 27, 2026, the BedrockConnect helper may stop reaching servers until it adds support for Bedrock's new way of connecting. That part is run by the BedrockConnect project, not by us. If it stops working, check back here.

Stuck? Restart the console and Minecraft after changing DNS. If the featured server just opens normally with no menu, your home network may be using IPv6 or a content filter, so set the DNS on your router instead. On PlayStation, try 45.55.68.52 as the primary DNS. Set your DNS back to automatic later if you want.

Already play on Bedrock? Both ports are available

Both Bedrock ports are enabled: 25622 and 20795. Keep the port that works on your device. If you cannot join on 25622, try 20795 with the same address; if you cannot join on 20795, try 25622. Device and game-version compatibility can differ. Geyser explains the planned Bedrock 26.60 transport change; this is tied to your game update, not a simultaneous cutoff for every device. To edit the port on a phone, iPad, tablet or Windows PC:

  1. Play, then Servers. Scroll down to the servers you added.
  2. Pick Wink Reyes Valley, then Edit. On a touch screen, tap it and then Edit, or press and hold it.
  3. Leave the address as it is (the new short wrv.duckdns.org and the older addresses all work). Change only the Port to the other Bedrock option (20795 or 25622), then Save.
  4. Join. The first time, a Trust on First Use? box pops up. Tap Trust. You only do this once on each device.

Does the old Bedrock port still work? Yes, again. It was off from about 12:37 AM to 7:09 PM Central on October 26, 2026, and it is back on to restore an alternative for devices having trouble. Bedrock works on both 25622 (new way) and 20795 (old way). If Bedrock still will not connect on one port, try the other (20795 or 25622) without changing the address. Keep a working saved entry. Keep your saved address; wrv.duckdns.org and the older addresses all work. Consoles that join through BedrockConnect can use either port.

Java players change nothing: Java stays on port 20795, now at wrv.duckdns.org:20795 (the older addresses also work).

If the game feels slow or choppy

  1. Open Settings, then Video. Set Graphics to Classic or Fast (not Fancy or Vibrant).
  2. Find Render Distance and slide it lower, to about 6 to 8.
  3. Close other apps running on your phone, iPad, tablet, or computer.

Still slow? Close the game and open it again.

The Five Seals: the Road to the Guild

Long ago, in the days of the Adventurers Guild, magic flowed free through the valley. It fed everything it touched: the fields, the tools in a farmer's hands, and the monsters in the dark. Fed on magic, the monsters of that age were far stronger than the ones you know today.

Read the whole story: the five keys and what each seal opens

That age ended. The world was sealed. Magic drained away, the guild's door was locked, and the monsters shrank to the shadows you fight now. But seals do not hold forever. Magic is seeping back into the valley, and the Old Quartermaster's warning stands over everything below: the magic returning now is stronger than the magic of his era.

The Old Quartermaster served the guild in those days. He stands in the spawn village today, an old man by a lectern of tales, because he remembers how this story goes. Five seals hold the guild. Each one falls to a family victory, and progress is shared: a victory earned by anyone counts for everyone. As each seal falls, more magic returns to the world — spells the family can cast, and strength in the wild things. That is the bargain of the road: every door you open, opens for the monsters too.

The valley today: 1 of 5 seals broken

  • The guild is LOCKED. The Adventurers Guild hall stands built but sealed. The guild button and /ag show the quest page instead, and /seals in game shows what remains.
  • Magic is awake. Seal 1 broke on October 23, 2026, at about 5:46 PM Central, when the family won a raid under Bad Omen V. The Healer's school of supportive magic is open: get your first wand from the Old Quartermaster in the spawn village.
  • Night has come back. The valley's long day ended on October 18: the sun rises and sets again, and the dark belongs to the monsters once more. Plan your exploring around the night.
  • The wild has an edge. About 7 of every 100 wild monsters rise as elites, and they feel like the Quartermaster's stories: since the Seal 1 step went in on October 25, 2026, they hit about 75% harder than normal (it was about 60% before), and they take about 45% less damage from your weapons — every hit lands at about 55% of its usual strength. An elite is matched to the strength of the player it fights, so every fight is winnable — and running away is always allowed. Only wild, natural monsters can rise as elites; spawner and farm mobs never do.
  • The Quartermaster is waiting. Tap him in the spawn village and he tells his tales, one at a time. He speaks them aloud now, in Briggs's voice: in a dialogue box on phones, iPads, tablets and consoles, and on computers too, once you accept the small voice pack the game offers. Since the first seal fell, he is the one who hands out the first wands.

The five keys

The seals fall to five great victories. They can be won in any order: the first victory breaks Seal 1, the second breaks Seal 2, and so on, whichever they are. How each fight goes is for the family to learn by facing it. Here is where each one waits and how to start it.

The Ender Dragon

She waits in the End. Find a stronghold, fill the End portal frame with eyes of ender, and step through.

The Wither

You summon it yourself: four soul sand in a T shape with three wither skeleton skulls on top. Skulls drop from wither skeletons in Nether fortresses.

The Elder Guardians

They live deep inside an ocean monument. Find a monument in a deep ocean and go in.

The Warden

It sleeps in the Deep Dark, around the ancient cities far underground. The sculk shriekers there call it up.

A Raid Victory

Drink an ominous bottle of Bad Omen V, the strongest omen, then walk into a village. That starts the raid that counts for this seal.

Your personal boss rewards

Installed October 28, 2026, about 8:44 PM Central: the team-reward files are installed and server startup completed. Real Java/Bedrock player checks are still pending.

Join your family near the boss while its fight is active. You can qualify by being there to help, including as a healer; you do not have to deal damage. Arriving after the boss drops below 70% health still counts. Creative and spectator observers do not qualify. Once you qualify, dying, respawning, disconnecting or reconnecting does not erase your personal reward if the team earns a legitimate win. Standing near an idle boss is not a completed win; the separate combat and anti-cheese requirements still apply.

For the first eligible win of each remaining seal, every qualified teammate gets their own named signature item: Gravewhisper from the Wither, Monument's Answer from the elder trio, Hushwalkers from the Warden, or First Breath from the dragon. The existing once-per-seal signature limit remains: repeat wins do not create another set, and old completed signatures are not awarded retroactively. The boss's shared material loot stays one pool, not one copy per player. Raid rewards and boss difficulty are unchanged.

Use /bossrewards to collect your personal claim. The installed Java and Bedrock compass update adds My Boss Rewards; real-client menu acceptance is still pending. A nearby owner-only chest marker is attempted for each eligible player in the normal Overworld, Nether or End where placement is safe. It does not overwrite blocks or place in protected claims; unsafe locations, offline players or an existing owner marker use the personal reward menu instead. The guild world is excluded.

The world chest is a shortcut to your claim, not a container holding the reward. Losing the marker does not erase an unclaimed reward. Your claim stays available while you are offline or your inventory is full. Close your wand hotbar before claiming so the item goes into your normal inventory. If a claim says it needs review, contact Mike rather than repeatedly trying to force another copy.

Source instructions and draft records: BossTrial team rewards and matching Bedrock menu. These source records remain draft and unmerged; installation was verified separately.

Verification limits: real-client compass screens, owner-only access and genuine-fight late-arrival/death/rejoin and inventory handoffs still need player acceptance. This update does not grant seals, spawn a test boss or change Magic or Maker's Mark.

What each seal releases

Every fallen seal changes the world twice: once for the family, and once for the wild. Tap a seal to see both halves.

Seal 1 · Magic WakesBroken October 23, 2026, about 5:46 PM Central (raid victory under Bad Omen V)

For the family: the first magic returns. The Old Quartermaster hands out the first wands in the village, and the Healer's school of supportive magic opens: 43 spells in the world, including the healer's kit (Mend, Flash Heal, Ward and Greater Heal, added October 24, 2026) and Storm Shackle, a chaining lightning spell made just for this valley. Full spell list: the Magic skill tree.

For the wild: the returning magic feeds the monsters too, and this half arrived early: elites already rose about 7 of every 100 wild spawns before the first seal fell. Now that this seal has fallen, elites hit harder still: about 75% above normal.

Seal 2 · The PathfinderSealed · breaks with the family's second victory

For the family: the Pathfinder's school opens and the spellbook grows to 93 spells: travel, finding, and getting home again.

For the wild: elites rise about 8 of every 100 wild spawns and hit about 90% harder than normal, and your hits land softer on them, at about 50% of their usual strength. Today, with Seal 1 broken, elites hit about 75% harder (that step went in on October 25, 2026), so Seal 2 is the next step up.

Seal 3 · The WonderSealed · breaks with the family's third victory

For the family: the Wonder school opens and the spellbook grows to 161 spells, the strangest and showiest magic of the road.

For the wild: both halves move. Elites rise about 9 of every 100 wild spawns, and elite damage rises to about 105% above normal; elites stand longer against you too: your hits land at about 50% of their usual strength.

Seal 4 · The MasonsSealed · breaks with the family's fourth victory

For the family: every school of magic is open: 202 spells in the world. The Masons' building magic goes to every mage (their conjured blocks are temporary and respect land claims), and the last schools open with it, weather and time and the great Master spells.

For the wild: the hardest nights yet. Elites rise about 10 of every 100 wild spawns and hit about 120% harder than normal, and your hits land at about 45% of their usual strength.

Seal 5 · The Guild OpensSealed · breaks with the family's fifth and final victory

For the family: the last seal falls and the Adventurers Guild opens its doors: /ag to the guild hall, its own world of NPCs, shops, quests, and arenas like the Wood League. Your magic is already complete from the fourth seal, so this seal is all about the Guild.

For the wild: the flood arrives with the open doors. More elites rise, about 12 of every 100 wild spawns, but they stay at their Seal 4 strength: they hit about 120% harder than normal and your hits land at about 45% of their usual strength.

Watch it happen in game: type /seals any time to see which seals stand and which have fallen. When a seal falls, a narrator's voice tells everyone online what opened, and anyone who was offline hears their missed beats once, on their next join. The road is shared, so every victory moves the whole valley.

The Phantom Debt: why phantoms are back

Phantoms are back. While the Ender Dragon lives, phantoms return to the valley on Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Thursday nights. Friday, Saturday and Sunday nights are safe: no phantoms come on weekends. This is the Phantom Debt, and it repeats every week until the dragon falls. The Phantom Debt is live now: it began on the evening of October 18, 2026 (ExcelNoPhantoms 1.2.0, event mode). The first haunted night was Monday, October 19, 2026.

Read more about the Phantom Debt

Why are phantoms back? What is the Phantom Debt? The valley owes a debt for its long, safe days. While the dragon lives, her nightmares leak into the sky, and the phantoms ride them down on haunted nights: Monday night, Tuesday night, Wednesday night and Thursday night. The guild's powers are trying to protect the valley, and their wards hold on Friday, Saturday and Sunday — that is why weekends are safe. But the wards are not strong enough to hold all week, so the debt comes due again every Monday night.

When do phantoms come? Phantoms come at night on Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Thursday. Are there phantoms tonight? If tonight is a Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday or Thursday night, yes: it is a haunted night, and a warning goes out at dusk on haunted evenings so nobody is caught by surprise. On the night before the first haunted night, every player who joined the server was greeted with the warning in chat, so nobody missed the story. If tonight is Friday, Saturday or Sunday, sleep easy: the valley is safe and no phantoms come.

Phantom rules are the vanilla rules. Phantoms hunt players who have not slept in days: the longer you go without sleeping, the more phantoms come for you. Sleeping is the shield. Sleep in a bed and sleeping resets your sleepless nights, and the phantoms leave you alone. How do I stop phantoms? Sleep. Sleeping stops phantoms from hunting you, every time. Sleeping is how you keep phantoms away: a player who sleeps is a player the phantoms cannot hunt.

On haunted nights the sky tells the story itself, in chat, a line at a time through the night: the Long Day debt, the dragon's nightmares, the wards failing on haunted nights and holding on weekends, and sleep as the shield. Why does the sky talk at night? The sky talks because it is repeating the reason, so nobody forgets why the phantoms are back. What are the messages in chat at night? Those chat messages are the sky's own messages, not player messages: the sky retelling the Phantom Debt story, one message at a time. Read the messages in chat and you hear the whole reason. If you want to hear why, just stay up on a haunted night and listen.

When do the phantoms stop? When does the Phantom Debt end? The Phantom Debt ends for good when the Ender Dragon falls. The dragon has her own seal: defeat the Ender Dragon and the debt is paid, the nightmares end, and phantoms go back to being turned off every night, forever. Until that family victory, the cycle repeats: haunted Monday to Thursday nights, safe weekends, and sleep as your shield.

Plugin guide

Plugins are add-ons that give the server extra features. Here is what each one does, in plain words. Tap a name below to read about it.

Open the plugin guide (mcMMO, Jobs, shops, land claims, menus and more)

First, what is a command?

A command is a short message you type in the chat box. It always starts with a slash, like this: /spawn

To open the chat box on a computer, press the T key. On a phone, iPad or tablet, tap the speech bubble at the top of the screen. On a console, press right on the D-pad. Type the command and press Enter or Send.

When you see something in pointy brackets, like <name>, swap it for the real thing. So /tpa <name> becomes /tpa Grandma. Don't type the brackets.

The Valley Guide: answers in game chat

The Valley Guide is the server's in-game helper (ExcelGuide 0.3.2, live October 25, 2026, about 11:18 PM Central; knowledge refreshed October 26). The Guide answers questions in chat using this website's own knowledge, the same pages the "Ask the site" button reads.

Three ways to ask the Guide: type /ask and your question, start a chat message with “Guide,”, or ask about the server in ordinary chat and the Guide may answer. The Guide knows joining, the five seals, shops, claims, skills, and your own stats (it only ever tells you your own). When the Guide does not know, it says so instead of guessing, and the question is logged so its knowledge can grow.

The Guide can think now. Ask a question its cards do not cover and it composes a real answer on the spot, out of the valley's own knowledge, wording the answer for your question instead of reading a card aloud. It is a small AI that has only ever been taught this valley: this website's knowledge, and nothing else. Ask about yourself and the Guide answers with your real numbers, pulled from the server itself: your claim blocks, your skills, your balance. Ask who leads a board and it points you to the leaderboard first, and names who is on top. And the Guide learns. When it works out a good answer, it remembers that answer: ask the same question again and the Guide answers instantly, from what it learned, without having to think it through twice. Answers the family asks for again and again can be made permanent, so the Guide keeps getting smarter the more the valley talks to it. Does the Guide use AI? Yes: the Guide has an AI now, a small AI taught only this valley. The Guide uses that AI to answer from the valley's own knowledge, and it uses nothing else. If it truly does not know, it still says so instead of guessing, and the question is logged so its knowledge can grow.

New in 0.2.3: the Guide answers claim questions properly. Ask how many claim blocks you have and it answers with your own numbers, explains the rule, and points you to /claimslist to see them. The Guide never meets a direct question with silence, either: if an answer needs a moment, or a short wait applies, it tells you so and names the wait. And the Guide learns from the family now. When it does not know something and a player answers in chat, the Guide remembers that answer and serves it later with the credit attached, naming the player who figured it out. If two answers disagree, the Guide weighs them against this website's knowledge and what other players confirm; the answer that holds up is the one that gets learned, and anything it cannot settle is saved for Mike's weekly review.

Type /guide off if you do not want the Guide listening.

Server tips in chat

New: server tips in chat. Every 10 minutes the server shares a short tip in chat — handy commands and things that pay off, like /excelparty, claims, the shops, and the road to the guild. They start with [Tip] and rotate in order, so you’ll see them all over a long session. (The tips come from the AutoBroadcaster plugin; only admins can change the list.)

What are the chat tips? Why does the server post tips? The tips rotate every 10 minutes with a [Tip] prefix, in order. Their content covers party play, group hunts, claims, both shops, the Guide itself, /menu, and the five seals — small reminders of the commands and habits that pay off, posted so nobody has to ask twice.

How to use your wand

Your first wand from the Old Quartermaster knows Healer spells: Heal, Boon, Feed, Extinguish and Aqualung. Cure comes later when your wand ranks up to Student: casting spells earns spell points, and at 800 the wand moves up by itself (since October 26, 2026). (Wand controls updated October 23, 2026 so phones, tablets and controllers can switch spells fast, even in a fight.)

Java (mouse and keyboard)

  • Cast: left-click.
  • Next spell: right-click. It switches right away, no menu.
  • Spell list: sneak (Shift) and right-click, then click a spell.
  • Back one spell: press Q.
  • See your spell: its name shows above your hotbar and on the wand.

Bedrock touch (phone or tablet)

  • Cast: tap the air or a mob.
  • Next spell: press and hold on the screen. It switches right away.
  • Spell list: crouch, then press and hold. Tap a spell.
  • See your spell: its name shows above your hotbar.
  • Tip: if press and hold breaks a block, look at the sky first.

Bedrock controller (Xbox, also on iPad)

  • Cast: press RT (right trigger).
  • Next spell: press LT (left trigger). It switches right away.
  • Spell list: crouch (press the right stick) and press LT. Move to a spell and press A.
  • See your spell: its name shows above your hotbar.

Pick your controls: /wandstyle (new October 24, 2026, more styles October 25, every spell on the Tap bar October 26). The steps above are the Classic controls, and everyone starts on Classic. Type /wandstyle to pick a style. Each style's button says what crouch (sneak on a computer) does in it. Classic: the steps above. Sneak bars (phones, consoles and, since October 26, computers too): right-click opens your spell bar and a sneak press flips to the next bar. Cycle: step to the next spell. Tap to pick (the first wand setup, called Tap to cycle before October 26): tap your wand and your spells fill the hotbar; tap a spell icon to pick it (a tap never casts), then press and hold the screen on a phone, or left-click on a computer, to cast it. Crouch flips pages, and holding the wand slot, the empty slot, Q or right-click on a computer gives your items back. The existing wand-positioning behavior can move a wand to slot 9; that is separate from the flexible empty-exit rule below.

Tap bar: getting your normal items back. Select your wand to open the spell hotbar. Tap an ability icon to cast. To return to your normal hotbar, select an empty hotbar slot. Every Tap bar page is designed to keep one wand slot and at least one empty exit slot, with no more than seven abilities. The exit does not have to be slot 8, and the layout supports the wand in any hotbar position. Abilities that do not fit on a page are kept on other pages or in your Spell Book, not deleted.

Change which abilities are on the bar: open Server Menu → Spell Book → My spell bar (change it), or use /spellbar (also /mybar). Choose a bar slot to move, remove or replace an ability. Bar-slot numbers are your ability-loadout positions, not a promise that the same number is the physical empty exit slot. The bar holds up to 21 chosen abilities; other known spells remain in Spell Book.

Pages and spell information: crouch is the page action when another ability page is available. Use /spellbook or the server-menu Spell Book button to read spells and progress; /spellinfo opens spell information. Do not rely on tapping the already-selected wand icon or crouch + hold to open Book or exit on a phone: those gestures did not work in the reported phone tests and are not claimed fixed.

Current check: ExcelWandBars 0.3.3 was installed October 27, 2026. The owner reported the available-ability test worked well enough, but did not have enough unlocked abilities to fully test multiple pages. Full page/rearrangement acceptance is deferred until Seal 2. The green bar shows normal Minecraft XP; the separate blue bar above shows mana.

On a computer, right-click or Q remains the existing Tap bar exit control. On a phone or console, /wandstyle opens the style selector, and Spell Book has a Wand controls button. On a computer, the compass has a Wand controls button too. Your style choice is saved across rejoining, respawning and getting a new wand. See how wand SP and ability ranks work.

Spells use mana. While you hold your wand, a thin blue bar at the top of the screen shows your mana as a number, like "Mana 42" (since October 26, 2026). Wait a few seconds if a spell will not cast; the number counts back up as your mana refills. The wand is bound to you, so you can't lose it.

Classic controls only: right-click (Java), press and hold (touch) or press LT (controller) to switch to the next spell; crouch and use that control to open the spell list. These are not Tap bar instructions. If you use Tap bar, follow the ability icons and empty-slot exit above, and use /spellbook for the spell list.

Maker's Mark: who made or found your gear

Crafted by identifies the original tracked crafter. Found by identifies the first tracked person taking an eligible unsigned weapon after this update, including an older unsigned weapon from storage. It does not reconstruct historical ownership. Opening a chest alone does not claim its weapons, and passing an already signed item to another player does not replace its Crafted by or Found by name.

Your counters stay with the gear. Weapons keep kills and bosses; Kills currently includes both mobs and players. Armor keeps its existing Hits blocked counter, which counts qualifying hits, not an amount of damage prevented. Normal enchanting, renaming, repairs and upgrades are designed to preserve attribution and counters. Combining two tracked items keeps the higher value of each counter, not their sum. The item's ordinary vanilla recipe changes still apply.

Choose your settings: open the compass's Settings → Maker's Mark. Java uses a dedicated settings submenu; Bedrock uses the two-switch form. Or use /mark, /mark on, /mark off, /mark stats on or /mark stats off. Turning signatures off stops new signatures; it does not erase existing names. Gear stats has its own switch.

Magic wands and EliteMobs gear are excluded. Color milestones are not included, and this update adds no damage or armor bonus, effects or progression changes. Real Java/Bedrock menu and inventory-transfer acceptance still needs checking. Ambiguous transfers can remain unsigned rather than guessing a finder, and third-party plugins that replace all item metadata cannot be guaranteed to preserve it.

Your gear explains itself

New: your gear explains itself. Every enchanted item carries a short block under its own text saying what each of its enchantments does at that item's level, with the real numbers, and weapons also show their true total hit. The green 'Attack Damage' line still shows the item's base damage only — that's how the game draws it, not a bug: Sharpness adds its damage inside the hit itself (a Sharpness 5 diamond sword reads 7 there but hits for 10). Old gear picks up its explanation the next time you handle it; enchanting, the anvil and the grindstone keep it current.

Why does my sword say 7 attack damage? The green Attack Damage line shows the item's base damage only — that is how the game draws it, not a bug. Enchantments like Sharpness add their damage inside the hit itself, so a Sharpness 5 diamond sword reads 7 there but hits for 10. What does Sharpness do? Every level adds damage to each hit: a Sharpness 5 sword adds 3 damage on top of the sword's own. What do my enchantments do? Read the short block under the item's own text — ExcelItemInsight lists every enchantment on the item with what it does at that item's level and the real numbers. How much damage does my sword really do? Weapons show their true total hit in that block, base damage plus Sharpness, so the number you read is the number you hit for.

The family field guide

mcMMO: what will you unlock next?

Mine, fish, farm, fight, and get better as you play. Every skill has its own level, and you can work on all of them. You don't have to choose a class.

Some rewards happen automatically. Others are short boosts you switch on. Pick a skill below to see what it does, when it unlocks, and how to use it. For every perk on one page, see the Skill Trees.

Explore skillsUse an abilityUseful commandsPlay togetherXP bonuses
19 skills to explore

Gathering, fighting, and useful everyday skills.

Your levels matter

Unlocks use this server's Retro scale. Level 1000 is a milestone, not a hard limit.

Start with /mcstats

See your levels, then try a skill command such as /mining.

New here? Try Mining first.

Mine natural stone and ores. Double Drops starts at level 1; your first Super Breaker boost unlocks at Mining level 50. See the Mining card.

mcMMO skill XP is different from green Minecraft XP orbs and Jobs money. Placing and re-breaking the same blocks usually won't earn skill rewards.

Find your next skill

Automatic happens while you play. Active boost needs activating. Use it yourself needs a special action.

In combat, 2 damage points equal 1 heart. Let melee attacks fully charge for the strongest hits; armor can reduce the final damage.

Unlock levels below were checked against server files on October 12, 2026 (UTC). Your in-game skill command shows your current bonuses and any permission-based differences.

No skills match that search. Try an ability name, a shorter word, or choose All skills.

Mining Mine stone and ores for extra drops and powerful digging abilities. 6 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/mining shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Mine eligible natural stone and ores with a pickaxe.

  • Double Drops
    AutomaticLevel 1

    A chance for an extra copy of normal mining drops.

    Use: Passive while mining eligible blocks.

    Mine natural blocks. Placing and breaking the same blocks does not earn normal bonus drops.

  • Super Breaker
    Active boostLevel 50

    Mine faster for a short time. Successful bonus-drop rolls can give triple drops.

    Use: Right-click with a pickaxe, then hit an eligible mining block within four seconds.

    Wait: 4 minutes after it ends

    Triple drops happen when the bonus succeeds; every block is not guaranteed to drop three items.

  • Mother Lode
    AutomaticLevel 1000

    A high-level chance to receive triple mining drops, even outside Super Breaker. If it fails, the normal Double Drops roll still happens.

    Use: Passive while mining eligible blocks.

  • Blast Mining
    Active boostLevel 100

    Remotely detonate TNT for improved ore recovery, fewer ordinary debris drops, and Mining XP from destroyed ores. Higher ranks improve the ore bonus and can add bonus copies.

    Use: Place TNT, stand safely back, hold a pickaxe or flint and steel, sneak, aim at the TNT, and right-click.

    Wait: 1 minute

    Rank levels: 100 → 250 → 350 → 500 → 650 → 750 → 850 → 1000

    TNT explodes immediately. Stay back and keep it well away from builds, players and animals.

    Aim from a safe distance. The remote range is 100 blocks. Extra ore copies are chance-based.

  • Bigger Bombs
    AutomaticLevel 100

    Makes mcMMO-tracked Blast Mining explosions larger. It improves with your Blast Mining rank.

    Use: Automatic when Blast Mining is used.

    A larger blast damages a larger area. Use it only in a safe mining area.

  • Demolitions Expertise
    AutomaticEffective protection from level 500

    Reduces damage from your own Blast Mining TNT. Protection begins at level 500 with the checked settings.

    Use: Passive against your own tracked Blast Mining explosion.

    Rank levels: 500 → 750 → 1000

    Protection applies to your own Blast Mining TNT. It does not make ordinary TNT, creepers or other players’ explosives safe.

    More about this ability

    With the checked settings, protection is 25% at level 500, 50% at 750, and 100% at 1000, for your own tracked Blast Mining TNT only.

Sources for this skill

Woodcutting Chop trees faster and earn extra wood. 5 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/woodcutting shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Chop natural or grown logs with an axe.

  • Harvest Lumber
    AutomaticLevel 1

    A chance to gain an extra copy of a chopped block's drops.

    Use: Passive when chopping eligible blocks with an axe.

  • Tree Feller
    Active boostLevel 50

    Chopping a tree can bring down connected logs and nearby tree parts in one action. Harvest Lumber and Clean Cuts can still add drops.

    Use: Right-click with an axe, then hit a valid log within four seconds and chop it.

    Wait: 4 minutes after it ends

    Check axe durability first. A worn-out axe can make the ability fail and injure you.

    Be careful near treehouses and neighboring trees. Replant afterward. Some tree shapes or protected blocks prevent a complete felling.

  • Leaf Blower
    AutomaticLevel 150

    Instantly breaks leaves and eligible non-wood tree parts when hit with an axe.

    Use: Passive; hit the leaf or eligible tree part with an axe.

    Hold an axe to use it.

  • Knock on Wood
    AutomaticLevel 300

    Improves Tree Feller rewards. Rank 1 keeps sapling/propagule drops from more leaves; rank 2 adds a chance for Minecraft XP orbs from eligible non-log tree components.

    Use: Passive during Tree Feller.

    Rank levels: 300 → 600

    This improves Tree Feller; punching a tree does not trigger it. Sapling drops are still chance-based.

  • Clean Cuts
    AutomaticLevel 1000

    A high-level chance for triple woodcutting drops. If it fails, Harvest Lumber can still award double drops.

    Use: Passive while chopping eligible blocks; also works during Tree Feller.

Sources for this skill

Herbalism Harvest more food and learn helpful replanting tricks. 7 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/herbalism shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Harvest fully grown crops and eligible plants. Let them grow again before harvesting.

  • Double Drops
    AutomaticLevel 1

    Eligible harvests can give extra produce.

    Use: Passive when harvesting eligible mature plants.

    Harvest fully grown crops. Bonus drops depend on the plant; falling sections of tall plants do not all receive the bonus.

  • Green Terra
    Active boostLevel 50

    A short farming boost: bonus harvests can give triple drops, supported crops replant more reliably, and seeds can turn certain blocks green.

    Use: Right-click with a hoe, then hit or harvest an eligible plant or hit a convertible block within four seconds. While active, hit convertible blocks with wheat seeds available in your inventory.

    Wait: 4 minutes after it ends

    Keep the matching seeds or crop item in your inventory for replanting. Sneaking skips replanting.

    Greening a block consumes a wheat seed. Not every harvest is guaranteed to give triple drops.

  • Green Thumb
    AutomaticRanked benefits at level 250; basic replanting can happen earlier

    Harvest with a hoe to try automatically replanting supported crops. Higher ranks replant at a later growth stage. Wheat seeds can also turn certain blocks green.

    Use: Harvest with a hoe without sneaking; keep the matching seed/crop item in your inventory or offhand. Cocoa also supports an axe. For manual block conversion, right-click a compatible block while holding wheat seeds.

    Rank levels: 250 → 500 → 750 → 1000

    Crop replanting can happen before level 250, starting at the earliest growth stage. The level-250 unlock is for ranked growth benefits and manual block conversion.

    Keep the matching seeds or crop item in your inventory. Sneaking skips replanting. A failed manual greening attempt still uses its wheat seed.

  • Farmer's Diet
    AutomaticLevel 200

    Restores extra hunger when eating supported farm foods. Each rank adds one hunger point, up to five points (2.5 drumsticks).

    Use: Passive when eating supported foods.

    Rank levels: 200 → 400 → 600 → 800 → 1000

    Extra hunger does not remove the poison from poisonous potatoes.

  • Hylian Luck
    AutomaticAvailable from the start; improves with skill

    A small chance to find configured treasure when cutting certain plants with a sword.

    Use: Break a supported plant or flower-pot block with a sword.

    Use a sword on supported plants. Avoid decorative plants and flower pots around builds.

    There is no separate level gate, but the chance starts at zero and grows with your Herbalism level.

  • Shroom Thumb
    Use it yourselfAvailable from the start; improves with skill

    A chance to turn dirt, grass or a dirt path into mycelium.

    Use: Hold a red or brown mushroom and right-click a compatible block while carrying at least one of each mushroom.

    Every attempt consumes one red and one brown mushroom, even if it fails. The chance starts at zero and grows with your skill.

  • Verdant Bounty
    AutomaticLevel 1000

    A high-level chance for a successful bonus harvest to become triple drops outside Green Terra.

    Use: Passive during eligible harvests.

Sources for this skill

Excavation Find surprises while you dig. 2 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/excavation shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Dig eligible natural materials, such as dirt, sand and gravel, with a shovel.

  • Archaeology
    AutomaticLevel 1

    Digging can uncover configured treasures and extra Excavation XP. Higher skill levels unlock individual treasure entries; Archaeology ranks improve the Minecraft XP orb bonus when treasure is found.

    Use: Passive when digging eligible natural blocks with a shovel.

    Rank levels: 1 → 250 → 350 → 500 → 650 → 750 → 850 → 1000

    Different treasures need different digging materials and levels. This is a shovel skill, separate from Minecraft’s brush archaeology.

  • Giga Drill Breaker
    Active boostLevel 50

    Dig faster for a short time, with three chances for treasure and extra Excavation XP from eligible blocks.

    Use: Right-click with a shovel, then hit an eligible excavation block within four seconds.

    Wait: 4 minutes after it ends

    It does not dig a 3-by-3 area or triple every ordinary dirt and sand drop. It uses extra tool durability.

Fishing Catch treasures, get faster bites and learn fishing tricks. 6 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/fishing shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Fish with a rod. If warned about overfishing, move where your bobber lands.

Keep moving your fishing spot when warned about overfishing. Repeated catches in the same spot can stop earning fish or XP.

  • Treasure Hunter
    AutomaticLevel 1

    Fishing can reel in equipment, valuables and other configured treasures. Higher ranks shift the chances toward better rarity tiers and increase ordinary Minecraft fishing XP.

    Use: Passive when you successfully catch something with a rod.

    Rank levels: 1 → 250 → 350 → 500 → 650 → 750 → 850 → 1000

    A treasure normally replaces the fish you would have caught.

  • Master Angler
    AutomaticLevel 1

    Reduces the wait for a bite. Fishing while seated in a boat adds another small reduction.

    Use: Passive on casting a fishing rod; sit in a boat for the extra bonus.

    Rank levels: 1 → 200 → 300 → 400 → 600 → 700 → 800 → 900

    The wait still varies. A faster bite is not a guarantee of an immediate catch.

  • Ice Fishing
    Use it yourselfLevel 50

    Can open a small hole in ordinary ice so you can fish through it.

    Use: Cast onto qualifying ice and reel the grounded hook while aiming at the ice; the ability converts a small patch to water and recasts.

    Use ordinary lake ice. Packed ice and blue ice do not work. It can change a 3-by-3 patch, so avoid decorative ice.

  • Shake
    Use it yourselfLevel 150

    Hooking a supported creature can shake loose an item and award Fishing XP. Higher ranks improve its success chance.

    Use: Hook a supported living entity with the fishing rod and reel it in.

    Rank levels: 150 → 200 → 250 → 300 → 400 → 500 → 600 → 700

    Shake hurts the creature and can kill it. Avoid pets, villagers and family members.

  • Magic Hunter
    AutomaticLevel 200

    Some enchantable mcMMO fishing treasures can gain enchantments. Its enchantment odds improve with Treasure Hunter rank.

    Use: Passive when a suitable fishing treasure is found.

  • Fisherman's Diet
    AutomaticLevel 200

    Restores extra hunger when eating supported fish. Each rank adds one hunger point, up to five points (2.5 drumsticks).

    Use: Passive when eating cod, salmon, tropical fish, cooked cod or cooked salmon.

    Rank levels: 200 → 400 → 600 → 800 → 1000

    This does not make pufferfish safe to eat.

Sources for this skill

Swords Sword attacks gain bleeding, counter-attacks and a short splash-damage ability. 5 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/swords shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Deal damage with a sword to eligible mobs or players.

  • Rupture
    AutomaticLevel 1

    Sword hits can apply armor-bypassing damage over time. Rank chances are 15%, 33%, 40%, 66%; the duration is about 5 seconds.

    Rank levels: 1 → 150 → 750 → 900

    Let melee attacks charge fully for the best chance. Rupture cannot deliver the final killing blow.

  • Serrated Strikes
    Active boostLevel 50

    Temporarily adds nearby splash damage and attempts Rupture on splash targets. At full strength, splash is one-quarter of the triggering sword damage before armor, with a minimum of 1 damage.

    Use: Right-click with a sword to ready it, then hit a target within 4 seconds. Sneak while right-clicking if your off-hand is occupied.

    Wait: 4 minutes after it ends

  • Counter Attack
    AutomaticLevel 200

    While holding a sword, a successful counter damages a living melee attacker for half the incoming damage before armor. Chance increases with Swords level, up to 30% at level 1000.

    A counter hurts the attacker; it does not simply cancel the incoming hit.

  • Stab
    AutomaticLevel 750

    Adds +2.5 sword damage before armor at rank 1 and +4 at rank 2.

    Rank levels: 750 → 1000

  • Swords Limit Break
    AutomaticLevel 100

    Adds extra weapon damage before armor against tougher, better-armored players. Ten ranks; up to +10 damage before armor and other damage reductions at rank 10 against high-quality armor.

    Rank levels: 100 → 200 → 300 → 400 → 500 → 600 → 700 → 800 → 900 → 1000

    This is a player-versus-player bonus, not a bonus against monsters. PvP is off on this server, so it is not available in normal player combat. The numbers above describe the underlying mechanic, not an active PvP feature.

Axes Axe attacks gain extra damage, critical hits and crowd control. 6 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/axes shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Deal damage with an axe. Chopping trees trains Woodcutting instead.

  • Axe Mastery
    AutomaticLevel 50

    Adds +1 axe damage before armor per rank, up to +4.

    Rank levels: 50 → 100 → 150 → 200

  • Critical Strikes
    AutomaticLevel 1

    Chance to deal extra axe damage. At full attack strength, a critical is 2× damage against mobs or 1.5× against players. Chance reaches 37.5% at level 1000.

  • Armor Impact
    AutomaticLevel 1

    A 25% chance per armor piece to inflict extra durability damage, growing with rank. Base durability damage is 6.5 × rank before Unbreaking and rounding.

    Rank levels: 1 → 100 → 150 → 200 → 250 → 300 → 350 → 400 → 450 → 500 → 550 → 600 → 650 → 700 → 750 → 800 → 850 → 900 → 950 → 1000

    Damages armor durability rather than adding health damage.

  • Greater Impact
    AutomaticLevel 250

    Against an unarmored target, a 25% chance to add +2 damage before armor and strong knockback.

  • Skull Splitter
    Active boostLevel 50

    Temporarily splashes axe damage to nearby foes. At full attack strength, splash is half the triggering damage before armor, with a minimum of 1 damage.

    Use: Right-click with an axe to ready it, then hit a target within 4 seconds. Sneak while right-clicking if your off-hand is occupied.

    Wait: 4 minutes after it ends

    With an axe ready, hitting a creature triggers Skull Splitter; chopping a tree triggers Tree Feller.

  • Axes Limit Break
    AutomaticLevel 100

    Adds extra weapon damage before armor against tougher, better-armored players. Ten ranks; up to +10 damage before armor and other damage reductions at rank 10 against high-quality armor.

    Rank levels: 100 → 200 → 300 → 400 → 500 → 600 → 700 → 800 → 900 → 1000

    This is a player-versus-player bonus, not a bonus against monsters. PvP is off on this server, so it is not available in normal player combat. The numbers above describe the underlying mechanic, not an active PvP feature.

Unarmed Fight with empty hands and learn defensive tricks. 7 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/unarmed shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Deal damage with an empty main hand. Holding a random block does not count as empty-handed combat.

  • Steel Arm Style
    AutomaticLevel 1

    Adds punch damage across 20 ranks, from +1 damage before armor at rank 1 to +13.5 at rank 20.

    Rank levels: 1 → 100 → 150 → 200 → 250 → 300 → 350 → 400 → 450 → 500 → 550 → 600 → 650 → 700 → 750 → 800 → 850 → 900 → 950 → 1000

  • Berserk
    Active boostLevel 50

    Temporarily increases fully charged punch damage by 50% and instantly breaks certain weak materials, including excavation-type blocks, snow, and glass.

    Use: Right-click with an empty main hand, then punch a target or suitable block within 4 seconds. Sneak while right-clicking if your off-hand is occupied.

    Wait: 4 minutes after it ends

    Keep your main hand empty. Only certain soft blocks break instantly.

  • Arrow Deflect
    AutomaticLevel 200

    Chance to cancel an incoming arrow hit while your main hand is empty. Chance reaches 50% at level 1000.

    Keep your main hand empty. This blocks arrow damage; it does not shoot the arrow back.

  • Disarm
    AutomaticLevel 250

    Punches can knock a player’s held main-hand item onto the ground. Chance reaches 33% at level 1000.

    This mechanic affects players, not monsters. PvP is off on this server, so Disarm is not an available player-combat perk.

  • Iron Grip
    AutomaticListed rank: level 600; protection can happen earlier

    A chance to keep your held item when another player tries to Disarm you. The chance rises with Unarmed level, reaching 100% at level 1000.

    The listed first rank is level 600, but the underlying mechanic can protect earlier in this build. PvP is off on this server, so this player-combat protection is listed for reference.

  • Block Cracker
    Use it yourselfUse with Berserk from level 50

    While Berserk is active, punching supported brick blocks turns them into their cracked variants.

    Use: Activate Berserk, then punch stone bricks, infested stone bricks, deepslate bricks/tiles, polished blackstone bricks, or nether bricks.

    Use while Berserk is active, so its practical starting point is level 50. Be careful around brick builds.

  • Unarmed Limit Break
    AutomaticLevel 100

    Adds extra weapon damage before armor against tougher, better-armored players. Ten ranks; up to +10 damage before armor and other damage reductions at rank 10 against high-quality armor.

    Rank levels: 100 → 200 → 300 → 400 → 500 → 600 → 700 → 800 → 900 → 1000

    This is a player-versus-player bonus, not a bonus against monsters. PvP is off on this server, so it is not available in normal player combat. The numbers above describe the underlying mechanic, not an active PvP feature.

Archery Improve bow damage and recover some arrows. 4 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/archery shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Shoot eligible targets with a bow. Longer shots award a distance XP multiplier, capped at 50 blocks; fully drawn bow shots are also favored by the XP calculation.

This skill has passive bonuses; there is no usable right-click super ability for it in this version.

  • Skill Shot
    AutomaticLevel 1

    Adds 10% of the original arrow damage per rank, up to +200% at rank 20, limited to +9 extra damage before armor per hit.

    Rank levels: 1 → 100 → 150 → 200 → 250 → 300 → 350 → 400 → 450 → 500 → 550 → 600 → 650 → 700 → 750 → 800 → 850 → 900 → 950 → 1000

  • Arrow Retrieval
    AutomaticLevel 1

    Chance for arrows fired into a target to be returned as ordinary arrow drops when it dies. Chance reaches 100% at level 1000.

    Infinity arrows are excluded. Recovered arrows are ordinary arrows.

  • Daze
    AutomaticAvailable from the start; improves with skill

    Bow hits can disorient a player, change their view angle, add 10 seconds of nausea, and add +4 damage before armor. Chance reaches 50% at level 1000.

    Affects players, not monsters. The chance starts at zero and grows with Archery level. PvP is off on this server, so Daze is listed for reference rather than as an available player-combat perk.

  • Archery Limit Break
    AutomaticLevel 100

    Adds extra weapon damage before armor against tougher, better-armored players. Ten ranks; up to +10 damage before armor and other damage reductions at rank 10 against high-quality armor.

    Rank levels: 100 → 200 → 300 → 400 → 500 → 600 → 700 → 800 → 900 → 1000

    This is a player-versus-player bonus, not a bonus against monsters. PvP is off on this server, so it is not available in normal player combat. The numbers above describe the underlying mechanic, not an active PvP feature.

Taming Train animal companions, especially your tamed wolves. 10 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/taming shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Tame animals and let your own tamed wolves fight while you are online. Ordinary breeding does not award Taming XP.

Most combat bonuses apply to your own tamed wolves, not every animal. Summoned companions are temporary.

  • Beast Lore
    Use it yourselfLevel 1

    Inspect a tameable animal’s health and owner; horse-like animals can also show speed and jump information.

    Use: Left-click a tameable animal while holding a bone. The successful inspection cancels that attack’s damage.

  • Call of the Wild
    Use it yourselfLevel 1

    Summon a temporary companion: 10 bones for a wolf, 10 raw cod for a cat, or 10 apples for a horse. You can have two summoned wolves, one cat and one horse at a time.

    Use: Crouch and left-click air or a block while holding at least the required item amount in your main hand.

    These companions are temporary: they disappear after four minutes. The summoning ingredients are consumed.

  • Environmentally Aware
    AutomaticLevel 100

    Protects your wolf from falls and helps it escape hazardous blocks by teleporting it back to you.

    It can cancel falls and help a wolf escape hazards, but does not guarantee rescue from lethal lava or fire damage.

  • Gore
    AutomaticLevel 150

    Your wolf’s attack gains another copy of its base hit damage, before other bonuses and armor.

  • Pummel
    AutomaticLevel 200

    Your wolf has a 10% chance to knock back its target.

  • Fast Food Service
    AutomaticLevel 200

    On a wolf’s attack, a 50% chance to heal it by the hit’s original damage amount, up to its maximum health.

  • Thick Fur
    AutomaticLevel 250

    Halves incoming melee/projectile damage to your wolf and extinguishes its burning effect on a fire-tick event.

    This is damage reduction, not immunity to every kind of damage.

  • Holy Hound
    AutomaticLevel 350

    Adds health when your wolf takes magic, poison or wither damage, which can help offset the hit.

    The original magic damage can still apply. Do not rely on this as immunity or guaranteed net healing.

  • Shock Proof
    AutomaticLevel 500

    Reduces your wolf’s explosion and lightning damage to one-sixth of its previous amount.

  • Sharpened Claws
    AutomaticLevel 750

    Adds +2 damage before armor to your wolf’s attacks.

Sources for this skill

Acrobatics Improve your chance of reducing fall and attack damage. 3 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/acrobatics shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Gain XP from surviving meaningful falls and successful combat dodges against mobs. Repeating falls at the same spot or grinding the same mob is restricted by anti-farming checks.

Practice safely. These abilities do not make every fall survivable.

  • Roll
    AutomaticAvailable from the start; improves with skill

    A chance to reduce fall damage by up to 7 damage points (3.5 hearts), after normal fall-damage reductions. Chance scales to 100% at level 1000.

    Use: Automatic when landing from a damaging fall, without sneaking.

    Do not jump from lethal heights to test it. A roll can fail, and cannot save a fall that remains fatal after the reduction.

  • Graceful Roll
    AutomaticSneaking version of Roll; no separate unlock

    Sneaking doubles Roll’s success chance and doubles the maximum reduction to 14 damage points (7 hearts). With configured scaling it reaches 100% chance at level 500.

    Use: Hold sneak when you land from a fall.

    Sneak before landing. It still cannot save a fall that remains fatal after the reduction.

  • Dodge
    AutomaticLevel 1

    Chance to halve incoming attack damage, with a minimum of 1 damage still taken. Chance scales to 20% at level 1000.

    Does not work while blocking or if the reduced hit would still be fatal.

Sources for this skill

How to level up: Damage eligible targets with crossbow arrows. The combat path uses the distance-XP multiplier too.

This skill has passive bonuses; there is no usable right-click super ability for it in this version.

  • Trick Shot

    Arrows that glance off blocks can ricochet. Higher ranks allow more bounces.

    Use: Shoot a block at a glancing angle; a square hit does not ricochet on the first bounce.

    Rank levels: 50 → 200 → 400

    The listed first rank is level 50, but an initial ricochet can occur earlier in this server build.

  • Crossbows Limit Break

    Rank levels: 100 → 200 → 300 → 400 → 500 → 600 → 700 → 800 → 900 → 1000

    This is a player-versus-player bonus, not a bonus against monsters. PvP is off on this server, so it is not available in normal player combat. The numbers above describe the underlying mechanic, not an active PvP feature.

Tridents Add damage to thrown and melee trident hits. 2 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/tridents shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Damage eligible targets by striking with or throwing a trident.

This skill has passive bonuses; there is no usable right-click super ability for it in this version.

  • Impale
    AutomaticLevel 50

    Adds +1.5 damage before armor at rank 1, then +0.5 per additional rank, up to +6 at rank 10. Applies to melee and thrown tridents.

    Rank levels: 50 → 150 → 250 → 350 → 450 → 550 → 650 → 750 → 850 → 1000

    This is an mcMMO skill bonus, separate from the Impaling enchantment.

  • Tridents Limit Break
    AutomaticLevel 100

    Adds extra weapon damage before armor against tougher, better-armored players. Ten ranks; up to +10 damage before armor and other damage reductions at rank 10 against high-quality armor.

    Rank levels: 100 → 200 → 300 → 400 → 500 → 600 → 700 → 800 → 900 → 1000

    This is a player-versus-player bonus, not a bonus against monsters. PvP is off on this server, so it is not available in normal player combat. The numbers above describe the underlying mechanic, not an active PvP feature.

Alchemy Brew potions faster and learn new recipes. 2 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/alchemy shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Brew potions in a brewing stand.

  • Catalysis
    AutomaticAvailable from the start; improves with skill

    Your potions brew faster as your Alchemy level rises.

    Use: Automatic while doing the matching action.

    More about this ability

    Brewing speed rises from normal at level 0 to 4× at level 1000, before any special perks.

  • Concoctions
    AutomaticAvailable from the start; improves with skill

    Unlock extra brewing ingredients as you level up.

    Use: Brew the unlocked ingredient with the matching base potion, usually an Awkward Potion.

    Rank levels: 0 → 100 → 200 → 350 → 500 → 750 → 900 → 1000

    Some unlocked potions have harmful effects. Read the name before drinking. Hopper brewing is limited to first-rank recipes.

    Ingredients you unlock
    • Level 100: Carrot: Haste; slimeball: Dullness (mining fatigue); phantom membrane: Slow Falling
    • Level 200: Quartz: Absorption; rabbit’s foot: Leaping
    • Level 350: Apple: Health Boost; rotten flesh: Hunger
    • Level 500: Brown mushroom: Nausea; ink sac: Blindness
    • Level 750: Fern: Saturation
    • Level 900: Poisonous potato: Decay
    • Level 1000: Golden apple: Resistance

Repair Use materials to fix worn tools and armor. 3 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/repair shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Repair damaged equipment using mcMMO’s repair block. Each repair consumes one matching material.

How to use it: Hold the damaged item and use it on an iron block. Keep the matching repair material in your inventory.

Be careful: Repairing enchanted gear can remove or weaken its enchantments, even at high levels. Read the in-game confirmation before using valuable gear.

  • Repair Mastery
    AutomaticLevel 1

    Get more durability back from each repair material.

    Use: Automatic while doing the matching action.

    More about this ability

    The checked maximum adds 200% to the base repair amount at level 1000.

  • Super Repair
    AutomaticLevel 400

    A chance to double the amount repaired.

    Use: Automatic while doing the matching action.

  • Arcane Forging
    AutomaticLevel 100

    Gives a chance to keep enchantments while repairing. Higher ranks improve the odds.

    Use: Automatic while doing the matching action.

    Rank levels: 100 → 250 → 350 → 500 → 650 → 750 → 850 → 1000

    Before level 100, enchanted repairs can remove every enchantment. Higher levels still do not guarantee keeping all enchantments.

Salvage Take spare equipment apart and recover some materials. 2 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/salvage shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Salvage level is the average of Repair and Fishing, rounded down. Raise those skills to raise Salvage.

How to use it: Hold an unwanted item and use it on a gold block. Read the confirmation before proceeding.

Be careful: Salvaging destroys the equipment. Damaged items give fewer materials or can be too damaged to salvage. Enchantments may be lost.

  • Scrap Collector
    Use it yourselfLevel 1

    Recover materials from unwanted tools and armor. Higher ranks raise the material-return limit.

    Use: Automatic while doing the matching action.

    Rank levels: 1 → 100 → 150 → 200 → 250 → 300 → 350 → 400

    Salvaging destroys the item. Diamond equipment requires Salvage 50; netherite equipment requires Salvage 100.

    Worn items return fewer materials, and a nearly broken item may be too damaged to salvage.

    More about this ability

    Rank 1 returns up to one material. Later ranks allow up to twice the rank, limited by the item recipe and its remaining durability.

  • Arcane Salvaging
    AutomaticLevel 100

    A chance to recover enchantments on an enchanted book when salvaging enchanted equipment.

    Use: Automatic while doing the matching action.

    Rank levels: 100 → 250 → 350 → 500 → 650 → 750 → 850 → 1000

    Enchantments can be kept, weakened, or lost. The original item is still destroyed.

Smelting Make better use of furnace fuel and smelt extra resources. 3 abilities · Tap to see unlocks

/smelting shows your progress. Link to this skill

How to level up: Smelting level is the average of Mining and Repair, rounded down. Smelting eligible materials also splits mcMMO skill XP between Mining and Repair.

How to use it: Use a furnace normally. Click its inventory to associate it with you for mcMMO processing.

  • Second Smelt
    AutomaticAvailable from the start; improves with skill

    A chance to get an extra item from eligible furnace recipes.

    Use: Automatic while doing the matching action.

    More about this ability

    The checked chance reaches 50% at level 1000. Only configured outputs qualify, and there must be room in the output stack.

  • Fuel Efficiency
    AutomaticLevel 100

    Your furnace fuel lasts longer.

    Use: Automatic while doing the matching action.

    Rank levels: 100 → 500 → 750

    You must be online for fuel efficiency on the furnace associated with you. Only eligible smeltable materials qualify.

    More about this ability

    Ranks 1, 2, and 3 make fuel last 2×, 3×, and 4× as long.

  • Understanding the Art
    AutomaticFirst rank: level 100; extra XP begins at 250

    Raises the normal Minecraft XP you collect from eligible furnace results.

    Use: Automatic while doing the matching action.

    Rank levels: 100 → 250 → 350 → 500 → 650 → 750 → 850 → 1000

    Rank 1 keeps ordinary XP at 1×. The first actual boost, 2×, begins at level 250.

    More about this ability

    The eight ranks give 1×, 2×, 3×, 3×, 4×, 4×, 5× and 5× ordinary Minecraft XP.

How to use an active ability

  1. Reach the required level and hold the right tool.
  2. Use/right-click the tool to ready it. On a phone, iPad, tablet or controller, use your control scheme's Use / Interact action.
  3. Within 4 seconds, do the matching action: mine, chop, dig, harvest, or hit a target.
  4. Watch for the ability message. After the boost ends, most active abilities have a 4-minute base cooldown. Blast Mining has its own activation and a 1-minute cooldown.

The usual level-50 boosts start at about 3 seconds, grow to 12 seconds at level 500, and reach 22 seconds at level 1000. Player perks can change the timing.

Nothing happened?

Check your level with the skill command and your timer with /mccooldown. Make sure you're using the right tool and target. With something in your off-hand, try sneaking while you ready the tool. /mcability toggles ability activation, so check its message instead of repeatedly switching it.

Touch controls differ by Minecraft control scheme. A long press is not the same action on every phone, iPad or tablet.

Keep family builds safe. Tree Feller can take down connected logs, and Blast Mining explodes immediately. Practice far from homes, animals, and other players. Repair may remove enchantments; Salvage destroys the item.

Useful commands

  • /mcstats see all your skill levels
  • /mining see one skill; swap in /fishing, /repair, or another skill name
  • /mccooldown check when your active abilities are ready
  • /mcability turn ability activation on or off
  • /mctop see the skill leaderboard
  • /party info check your party and unlocked features

Server party (new): the family party that gives the +25% party bonus (Jobs money and XP, and mcMMO skill XP) uses /excelparty (also /eparty or /together), not /party. Try /excelparty invite NAME, /excelparty accept, /excelparty decline, /excelparty list, /excelparty leave, and (leader only) /excelparty kick NAME. The menu Party button does the same with taps. An invite also sends an mcMMO party invite. A party holds up to 5 players and is not saved over a server restart. Mike saw the Together x1.25 bonus bar work in game on October 16; the Java and Bedrock checks, the Jobs money bonus and shared party XP with real players are still unconfirmed. The always-on "party member in range" sign is live in ExcelTogether 0.3.0: the action bar above your hotbar shows your party range state and how many party members are in range, a title flash and a soft sound play when you come into range, and /excelparty cue off turns the flashes and sound off.

On Java, the compass menu's Skills & Perks button shows your mcMMO stats (the separate mcMMO Stats button was removed October 25, 2026), and /mcstats still works. On a phone or console, the server menu's Skills & Perks button (added October 20, 2026) opens the skill ladders in brief and the commands for checking your levels, so you no longer have to type /mcstats as your only in-game skills page. You can always use chat if a menu doesn't have that button.

Play together in a party

A party is a little team. Use Minecraft account names in commands, which can be different from display nicknames.

  1. One person types /party create Family (or chooses another team name).
  2. Invite someone with /party invite PlayerName.
  3. The invited player types /party accept.
  4. Check /party info together. Use /party quit to leave.

Party features unlock as the party's level rises: chat at 1, party teleport at 2, alliances at 5, item sharing at 8, and XP sharing at 10. These are party levels, separate from your skill levels. Features also depend on permissions; the compass teleport-request menu is still the easiest way to visit family.

How XP sharing works at party level 10

The party leader can use /party xpshare equal to share, or /party xpshare none to stop. Eligible teammates within 75 blocks share the earned skill XP pool.

With the checked settings, 100 earned XP shared by two nearby teammates becomes a 150-XP pool: 75 each, before individual modifiers. Sharing helps everyone progress together, but does not mean each player gets 150 XP.

What about the Together bonus?

The Together and Weekend XP update is installed. It adds bonuses to eligible mcMMO skill XP and green-bar XP, plus the /xpbonus indicator controls. Party XP sharing is a separate feature.

Startup and command registration are confirmed, with Together and the indicator enabled. The server owner reported the XP indicator working after personal testing on October 12. Identified-platform and detailed reward checks remain pending.

XP bonuses: how to use them

Installed October 12, 2026. The server is running mcMMO 2.3.003-WRV-XP-2 and ExcelWeekendXP 1.2.0 (formerly WeekendXP 1.0.2; renamed October 16, 2026; Friday added the same day). The instructions below describe this installed pair. Basic indicator behavior passed by owner report on October 12. The platform was not supplied; cross-platform and detailed gameplay acceptance checks remain pending.

Together: join the same mcMMO party and stay within 50 blocks of at least one other online teammate in the same world. Where enabled and permitted, eligible skill XP and green-bar XP receive 1.25×, or 25% more points. Party level 10 is not required for this separate Together bonus.

Weekend: Friday, Saturday and Sunday in Central time give 1.20×, or 20% more eligible points, including when you play solo. Both bonuses together give 1.50×, or 50% more points. The weekend check runs every 30 minutes, so a midnight change can take up to 30 minutes. Friday joined the weekend on October 16, 2026 (ExcelWeekendXP 1.2.0).

The update covers every normally earnable mcMMO skill. It boosts the skill you are actually using; it does not award every skill at once. Smelting and Salvage keep their parent-skill progression. These multipliers apply to XP points, not directly to the number of levels you gain.

Read the bonus bar

A separate bonus bar appears after positive accepted skill XP or eligible green-bar XP. It hides three seconds after your latest gain; earning more XP extends the same bar. Joining, standing still, or checking status does not open it. Server lag can stretch the three-second timer. ×1.00 means the base bonus factor. If skill XP and green-bar XP have different factors, it shows them separately. Restrictions or unavailable green-bar support are labeled instead of promising a reward.

  • /xpbonus or /xpbonus status shows your current factors, skill-specific rates, and relevant restrictions
  • /xpbonus hide disables future notifications and hides the current bar without turning off your XP rewards
  • /xpbonus show enables future brief notifications; earn eligible XP to see the next one
  • /xpbonus toggle switches future notifications on or off

Temporary indicator installed October 12: the three-second setting and startup were verified. The server owner personally tested it on October 12 and reported that it worked. This is a basic user-reported pass; identified Java/Bedrock coverage, precise timing and edge-case checks remain open. /xpbonus status still reports your factors in chat without opening the bar.

Created by us: ExcelWeekendXP (WeekendXP). Modified by us: Excel XP (mcMMO). These are website/GitHub labels for our weekend add-on and our changes to upstream mcMMO. The installed name of the weekend add-on is now ExcelWeekendXP (it was WeekendXP until October 16, 2026), and mcMMO keeps its name; the original mcMMO is by nossr50 and the mcMMO Team, who keep full credit for it.

Your shown/hidden preference is saved for later joins. If a factor is unexpected, check that your teammate is in the same party and world, is close enough, and that the status command does not show a permission or area restriction.

Which green-bar XP counts?

The bonus applies to eligible, positive XP-orb pickups after Mending. It boosts the points left for your green bar, not Mending repairs. Normal identified gameplay sources include mining, mob drops, fishing, furnace output, breeding, trading, bottles o' enchanting, and grindstones.

Direct command/API and advancement XP grants do not pass through this pickup bonus. Identified player-death refunds and unmarked custom or unknown orbs are excluded. Tiny fractional bonuses carry forward across eligible pickups instead of being lost.

Other plugins and merged XP orbs can affect source identification. This is not a guarantee that every refunded or custom orb can be distinguished. The owner-reported indicator pass does not verify every XP source, reward amount, or Java/Bedrock client; those checks remain open.

Full Together and indicator instructions on GitHub · WeekendXP instructions · Installation checks · XP source review

WeekendXP is a separate plugin. The Monday, October 12 startup check confirmed the weekday 1.00× base rate for both skill and green-bar XP. Actual weekend rollover, nearby-party rewards, Mending, and fractional pickups still need live gameplay checks. Use /xpbonus status for your current factors.

About this guide and its sources

Reviewed October 12, 2026 (UTC). Skill ranks, advanced settings, recipes, and treasure tables were compared with the server's files. Implementation was checked against mcMMO 2.3.003-SNAPSHOT source at commit 058279c.

The server uses Retro levels. A level number is an unlock, not a promise that a chance-based effect will happen every time. Player permissions, actual in-game behavior, and every touch/controller layout have not been play-tested. Use your skill command for your current stats.

Crossbows, Tridents, Maces, and Spears have working passive skills in the checked source. Their unfinished super abilities are not listed as unlocks. Older videos and wiki pages may describe different abilities.
