Back to the skills

Jobs: get paid for everyday things

What it does: Pick a job and you earn pretend money for doing normal things. A Miner gets paid for digging rock. A Farmer gets paid for harvesting crops. It's like a paycheck for playing.

There are 12 jobs: Brewer, Builder, Crafter, Digger, Enchanter, Explorer, Farmer, Fisherman, Hunter, Miner, Weaponsmith, and Woodcutter. You can join as many as you like.

Jobs pay server money and give job experience. The unused Jobs points currency is no longer earned or shown; old points balances are preserved. Money pay, job progression, and daily quests are unchanged. The separate Jobs item shop remains paused. Player-facing display checks are still pending.

Jobs reward changes and server settings on GitHub

What leveling up does: The more you work a job, the higher its level, and the pay goes up a little (about 1% more each level). Jobs give money and job experience for your job levels. That job experience is separate from mcMMO skills and Minecraft's green XP bar; Jobs do not give special powers.

What you can type:

  • /jobs browse see all the jobs and tap one to pick it
  • /jobs join <job> join a job, like /jobs join Farmer
  • /jobs leave <job> quit a job
  • /jobs stats see your job levels

In preparation: Skills and Jobs menu

Created by us: ExcelProgression. This beginner-friendly Skills/Jobs menu prototype is being tested and is not installed. A separate Jobs XP bonus draft is also not installed or ready to deploy: its mcMMO build passed, but the full Jobs build is still blocked. Current Jobs rewards have not gained those proposed bonus changes.

Third-party plugin: Jobs is by Zrips. We customize its settings here; the plugin itself is his work.

Essentials: getting around and money

What it does: Handy shortcuts. Jump back to the village, visit family (they tap Accept first), and check your money. There are no home teleports, so walk, ride, or sail back to your house.

Good to know: There are no free starter items. If you die, you drop everything you were carrying. Sleep in a bed and you should come back to that bed when you die (changed October 16; not yet tested with a real death).

What you can type:

  • /spawn go back to the village (or tap Spawn in the compass menu)
  • /compass get a new Server Menu compass if yours is missing
  • /tpa <name> ask to jump over to someone
  • /tpaccept or /tpdeny say yes or no when someone asks to come to you
  • /warps see the list of special places, and /warp <place> to go there
  • /places save your own named places, share them with family, and follow a compass to one
  • /msg <name> <message> send a private note to one person
  • /bal see how much money you have, and /balancetop see the richest players
  • /motd see the welcome message again

Third-party plugin: Essentials is EssentialsX, by the EssentialsX Team. EssentialsSpawn, in the plugin table below, is part of the same project.

GriefPrevention: put a fence around your stuff

What it does: Lets you claim land, like owning a yard. Nobody else can break or take anything there unless you say it's okay.

How to claim: Hold a golden shovel. Right-click (or tap) one corner of your land, then the opposite corner. That's your yard now. You earn more land to claim just by playing.

What you can type:

  • /trust <name> let someone build in your yard
  • /untrust <name> take that permission back
  • /claimslist see all your claimed land
  • /abandonclaim give up the claim you're standing in
  • /abandonallclaims give up all your claims at once

Build Flight: fly inside your own claim

Building something tall? Buy a Build Flight Pass in the Spawn Shop, under Travel & Animals: 30 minutes for $1,000 of Jobs money. A review screen shows first, and nothing is charged until you press Buy.

  • You can fly only inside your own claim. Make a claim with a golden shovel first. Family you /trust can't fly in your claim with your pass.
  • Double-tap jump to fly, and double-tap again to stop. On a phone or tablet, double-tap the jump button.
  • Leave your claim, get hit by a mob or an arrow, or start a fight, and flight turns off. You float down safely with no fall damage.
  • After about 10 quiet seconds with no fighting, if you are still in your claim, double-tap jump to fly again.
  • The 30 minutes keep counting, even during a fight or when you log off. Buy again to add 30 more minutes (you can hold up to 2 hours).
  • /buildfly shows how much time you have left. /buildfly help explains it in the game.
  • Flight is off in The End.

Third-party plugin: GriefPrevention is by the GriefPrevention team; the version we run is maintained by RoboMWM.

Claim blocks: how much land you can claim

The short version: claiming land is how you protect your stuff: inside your claims, nobody else can break blocks, open chests or take your things unless you trust them. Claiming spends claim blocks, and claim blocks are your land allowance. Every player starts with 100 claim blocks.

How you get more claim blocks: just play. You earn 100 claim blocks for every hour you are on the server, and the ones you earn from playing can build up to 80,000 at most. Claim blocks are never sold on this server: playing is the only way to get more.

Your first claim is automatic: the first chest you place claims a small patch of land around it (9 blocks across) so your starter things are safe. After that first chest, you claim land yourself.

How to claim land by hand: hold a golden shovel, right-click (or tap) one corner of the land, then the opposite corner. A claim has to cover at least 100 blocks of land. You can also type /claim to claim a patch of land around where you are standing.

How to check your claim blocks: you can check your own numbers any time. Type /claimslist to see all your claimed land. To see the numbers, open /leaderboard and pick Land Claimed: it shows how many blocks everyone has claimed and how many claim blocks they can still claim, including you.

Letting family in: /trust <name> lets someone build inside your claims, and /untrust <name> takes that back. The land stays yours either way.

Giving land back: stand inside a claim you don't want anymore and type /abandonclaim to give up just that one claim - that is how you delete a claim. To abandon all your claims at once, type /abandonallclaims. Either way you get all of that claim's blocks back, ready to spend on new land. The claim just stops protecting the land: anything you built there stays put, and when you delete a claim your buildings do not move. Want to try a different spot? You might claim the wrong corner now and then - abandon it and try again somewhere better. Nothing is lost by giving land back.

CoreProtect: the undo button

What it does: It quietly keeps a record of who placed and broke what. If something gets wrecked, Mike can see who did it and put it back. You don't need to type anything.

Third-party plugin: CoreProtect is by Intelli.

EliteMobs: tougher monsters and adventures

What it does: Some monsters are stronger than normal. They have a level number. What they pay is their coin shower — real dollars in your normal balance — plus their ordinary drops, and harder ones pay more. The Adventurer's Guild hall stands built, but it is sealed: type /ag anywhere and, while any of the five seals stands (1 of 5 broken today), you get the guild quest page instead of the hall. The hall itself — a separate world of NPC shops, guides and portals to adventures — opens when the fifth seal falls. When it does, talk to Hermes the transporter to leave, and stand perfectly still until you land — those teleports count down over a few seconds and cancel if you move. The stronger elite monsters you meet while exploring are still the everyday challenge. When an elite drops its coin shower, those coins are real dollars added to your normal balance, and higher-level elites pay more. Since October 21, 2026 the showers pay double what they paid before, so an elite hunt is worth noticeably more than it was. And in the Nether fortress, an elite wither skeleton beaten by a player drops its skull about 1 time in 4 — far oftener than an ordinary wither skeleton — so the surest road to the three skulls a Wither needs is hunting the elite ones. Ordinary wither skeletons keep the usual rare chance. Since the October 20, 2026 update (EliteMobs 10.9.10), the EliteMobs menus work better on phones, tablets and consoles: your /em status, quests and classes now open as proper dialog screens instead of inventory grids. Menus on a computer look the same as before, and shop screens stay inventory-style on every device. The setting is switched on and verified in the server’s own configuration; a first check of the new screens on a real phone is still to come.

Excel Party Drops: Hunting together pays off. When a tough elite monster falls, everyone in the fight nearby shares a fuller pile of drops — the more of the family in range when it falls (within about 50 blocks), the more drops, up to three times as many. Solo hunts pay exactly as before.

Watch the charge (our own add-on, ExcelZombieCharge): Hit a zombie, a husk, a drowned or a zombie villager — babies included — or a piglin with an arrow, a thrown trident or a magic spell, and it charges: half again as fast for about 3 seconds, and every new ranged hit refreshes the rush. A hit up close with a sword never provokes it. Sniping from a safe perch is what brings them running; standing your ground does not.

Tip: A higher-level monster is tougher. Bring good gear, or bring family.

Third-party plugin: EliteMobs is by MagmaGuy.

Shops: spend your Jobs money

Created by us: ExcelShops (WRVShops) and ExcelShopVillager (ShopVillager). ShopVillager is still the installed name of the villager shortcut.

Spawn Shop: open your compass menu and tap Spawn Shop (or tap the green "Spawn Shop" villager at spawn). Follow the Bedrock buying walkthrough below for ordinary items. The Veil Potion and two Death Protection options have their own one-item reviews.

Shop update (ExcelShops 1.3.2, October 20, 2026): the spawn shop now shows the same shelves at the same prices on every device — Java and Bedrock alike. If you play on a phone or console, everything Java players can buy is right there in the shop form, priced from the one catalog.

New shelves (October 20, 2026): the catalog grew from 18 offers to 45. Builder stone bundles (stone bricks, deepslate bricks and tiles, tuff bricks, in 64-stacks), the full music-disc shelf (21 discs, $500 for the classics up to $1,200 for Pigstep), and the first two Safe Blocks below. More shelves are coming in the next shop update: more builder families, banner patterns, decorative heads and dimmer light levels.

New at the spawn shop: Safe Blocks. A small shelf of blocks you can't get in survival, checked one by one to be pure decoration — they can't help you fight, mine, or skip ahead, and you can always take them back out of your build. Bedrock, barriers and End portal frames are never sold: once placed they can't be mined back, so they stay out of the shop for good.

  • Light Blocks ($75 each, sold in 4s for $300). Invisible light. Place one and the spot glows at full brightness (level 15) with nothing to see. Hold a light block in your hand to see where you've placed them. Heads-up: taking one back out does NOT give the block back — placing one spends it, like a torch that burns forever.
  • Petrified Oak Slabs ($240 for 64). An oak slab with stone manners: it won't burn, and you mine it with a pickaxe, not an axe (hands alone drop nothing, same as stone). Looks like wood, behaves like rock.

About symbols: decorative letter heads, when they arrive, render as blocks on every client, PlayStation included — but for chat-symbol products elsewhere on this site, some decorative symbols may not display on PlayStation.

Cosmetics at the spawn shop

Cosmetics at the spawn shop! Make your chat yours: 13 chat colors (Pink and Bubblegum Pink included), name effects like Rainbow Name and Sparkle Accents, and five archetype sets — Tank, Healer, Warlock, Firebender, and Lightning/Storm. Rent anything for a week (colors $200, effects $400, sets $600, Emote Pack $300) or own it forever at a flat whale price ($15,000 a color, $20,000 the Emote Pack, $25,000 an effect, $30,000 a set). Renting 4 weeks at once costs 3.5× one week ($700 / $1,050 / $1,400 / $2,100) in a single charge. Rentals end quietly — your chat just goes back to normal — and renewing stacks onto your remaining time. See what you hold and how long is left with /cosmetics. The Emote Pack adds /shrug, /hug, /tableflip, /unflip, /wave and /highfive. Note: some decorative symbols may not display on PlayStation.

How do I change my chat color? Buy a chat color in the Cosmetics category at the spawn shop, then see what you hold and equip it with /cosmetics. What are cosmetics? Cosmetics are sold at the spawn shop (the Cosmetics category): chat colors $200/week, name effects $400/week, archetype sets $600/week, the Emote Pack $300/week, and anything can be owned forever at a flat whale price ($15,000 a color, $20,000 the Emote Pack, $25,000 an effect, $30,000 a set), or rented 4 weeks at once for 3.5× one week ($700 / $1,050 / $1,400 / $2,100) in a single charge. Rentals stack when renewed and end quietly. /cosmetics shows what you hold, how long is left, and lets you equip. How much is pink chat? Pink is one of the 13 chat colors: $200 to rent for a week, $700 for 4 weeks, or $15,000 to own it forever. What does the Emote Pack do? It unlocks /shrug, /hug, /tableflip, /unflip, /wave and /highfive while you hold it.

Family shops (player shops): use the guided ExcelShops setup below. Stock your own claimed single chest, attach an ordinary sign, then set prices and confirm publication. No special sign syntax is needed.

ChestShop has been removed. Its old sign-format setup and Buy/Sell controls are no longer active. ExcelShops runs the native player shops and Spawn Shop; the separate Shopkeepers villager-trading plugin is unchanged.

ExcelShops player shops: review before you trade

Updated October 20, 2026. ExcelShops is running on its own (live version 1.3.2; the flow below was checked on 1.1); ChestShop has been removed. It uses readable native category and item lists on Bedrock, followed by the selected item’s description and quantity controls. Since 1.3.2 the phone and console shop is built from the same spawn catalog the computer shop browses: all 45 offers appear at the catalog prices on every device (the Bedrock shop used to carry only 21 products), and the catalog can now hold up to 250 offers. Java keeps its compact category inventories. Shop screens show your balance, cost, money left and any shortfall. Mike checked the new shop on his phone on October 14, 2026 and confirmed the flow works. A live purchase with money movement, and a second player buying from a player shop, have not been verified yet.

The custom add-on is now named ExcelShops. It runs the player shops (also called chest shops): shops you stock yourself so you can sell items to other players. Native Excel shops and Spawn Shop do not require the removed ChestShop plugin. Both use review and a separate final purchase confirmation. The Jobs item shop and two purchasing example menus are paused.

Selling to other players: stock a chest inside your claimed land, attach an ordinary sign, set your prices, and confirm publication. Other players browse your player shop, review their purchase, and tap Confirm; the money goes straight to your balance. You can sell almost any stuff you can stock: food, blocks, tools and enchanted books. Restock from the same screens when other players buy your stuff. Unrelated trading systems are separate; this guide does not claim that every possible server purchase route was player-tested.

Buy from an Excel shop chest
  1. Open a chest labeled [Excel Shop], or use its sign. Old managed [WRV Shop] labels also work. The browsing screen is read-only.
  2. Choose an item and how many you want. On a Bedrock phone, enter a whole number in the form. On Java, choose Enter Amount to type an exact whole number, then Use amount; plus/minus buttons remain available. See the typed-entry guide below.
  3. Review the item, quantity, unit price, and total. Check enchantments and custom item details too.
  4. Choose Confirm purchase once on the separate final confirmation screen. Back, Cancel, closing the menu, or disconnecting before confirmation does not buy anything.

Stay within six blocks. Quotes normally expire after 60 seconds. Changed prices or stock need a fresh review; insufficient money or inventory room prevents the purchase. If phone forms are unavailable, the inventory buttons are used. A recovery warning means stop and ask Mike, rather than repeatedly retrying.

Make your own Excel shop
  1. Place your own single normal or trapped chest in a land claim you own. Put your sale items inside. Both the chest and its sign must be in your claims; being trusted in someone else's claim is not ownership.
  2. Sneak while placing an ordinary wall sign against the chest, or a standing sign on top. Finish the normal sign editor. No special sign syntax is needed.
  3. Answer Make this a shop?, then set the price for one item for every distinct item type. Choosing No leaves ordinary storage.
  4. Review the owner, items, prices, and stock. Choose the separate Confirm and publish shop action to open it.

On phones, enter a positive unit price with up to two decimal places. Java has Enter Amount for an exact price such as 0.50, plus buttons for small adjustments. Review and confirm before publishing. Java input and cursor checks are still open. If a setup was canceled, look at the attached ordinary sign and use /excelshop setup to pick up where you left off.

Excel shop setup supports only your separately placed single chest. It does not adopt double chests, another player's recorded chest, or old ChestShop-formatted signs. Use an ordinary attached sign and the guided setup.

Restock, change prices, or close

Open your own Excel shop chest/sign, or look at it and type /excelshop manage. Choose Open stock chest to move your stock normally. New item types need a price before they appear for sale; sold-out listings can be restocked.

Edit item prices needs review and confirmation. Close shop also needs confirmation and returns it to ordinary storage with all stock still inside. Close it before breaking its chest or sign. Active Excel shop stock cannot be moved by hoppers.

If a shop is blocked after a claim change or server problem, ask Mike to check it. Don't grant public container trust as a workaround. The old /wrvshop command remains a working alias for /excelshop. Both the shop chest and its attached sign open the UI: owners get management, while other players get the purchase catalog.

The Spawn Shop

Use the Bedrock buying walkthrough for ordinary items, or the separate Veil Potion and Death Protection guides. No chat command is needed for normal shopping. /shop opens the categories; /excelshop keeps the standalone ordinary-item catalog as a fallback.

Five compact categories
  • Food: bread, steak, golden carrots and golden apples.
  • Building: torches, oak logs, glass, iron ingots and water buckets.
  • Travel & Animals: saddles, name tags, leads, ender pearls, fireworks and the Build Flight Pass.
  • Enchanting: XP bottles, Mending, Unbreaking III and Efficiency IV.
  • Potions & Protection: Veil Potion, Death Protection and +Death Protection Potion.

Java keeps each compact 3-by-3 menu with your balance and Back to Categories. Bedrock uses a labeled list instead. The category chooser has Back to Menu. Java quantity Back returns to the categories; Enter Amount Cancel keeps the current amount and final-review Back returns to quantity. Bedrock uses the item-specific controls below. Bedrock native-form Cancel/close stays closed. Browsing, Back and Cancel never purchase.

The five categories contain all 21 existing products: 18 ordinary bundle offers plus Veil Potion and both Death Protection choices. Travel & Animals also has the Build Flight Pass, which opens its own review screen before any money is taken. Every price and bundle size is unchanged: one 16-bread bundle costs $40, and two bundles give 32 bread for $80. Review the bundle size and final item count before confirming. Java display and input checks for the current shop are still open.

Bedrock shop controls (ExcelShops 1.1): read first, then choose the amount

Installed October 14, 2026. On a phone or console, each item opens its own details screen before you choose an amount. Mike checked this flow on his phone on October 14 and confirmed it works.

  1. Item details: choose a category, then an ordinary item labeled view details. The second line shows its bundle size and price. Selecting Steak opens Steak’s own details; it does not change Bread’s quantity or buy anything. Read the description, selected amount, total and projected balance. Use Enter exact amount if you want a different positive whole number; submitting returns to the same item’s details. More about exact amounts.
  2. Review this quantity: choose this button when the selected amount is right. It opens the purchase review without buying anything.
  3. Review purchase: check the item, bundle size, final item count, total and money left. Choose Continue to confirmation to continue. Change quantity returns to the same item’s details.
  4. Are you sure? is the separate final confirmation screen. Check the purchase once more; going Back or canceling does not buy anything.
  5. Choose the separate final Confirm purchase action once. Only this action can move money or items. If you do not have enough money or inventory room, the purchase is blocked.

Back and close: use Back to Food or the corresponding category button on item details. Native number entry has no normal Back button; closing number entry cancels safely. Item details return to the originating category; the category returns to the category chooser, and Back to Menu returns to the main menu. Cancel or native X closes safely. Old, repeated or expired responses cannot purchase. If native forms are unavailable, this mobile Spawn Shop flow closes with an explanation rather than opening unread inventory controls.

Veil and Death Protection: these three fixed-quantity products show their own details, then Open guarded review. Their existing review has its own Confirm; its Back returns to the shop’s category chooser. They have no quantity editor.

The native app controls button wrapping, scrolling and keyboard style. A numeric phone keyboard is not guaranteed. A live purchase with money movement, and a second player buying from a player shop, have not been verified yet.

Veil Potion: farm while standard mobs ignore you

ExcelMobVeil 0.1.0 is installed, October 13, 2026. The exact plugin, $2,000 price and new shop entry were verified after restart. Real Java/Bedrock screens, live purchases and the full live plugin combination remain unverified.

Veil Potion — $2,000 in-game money: open your compass menu → Spawn Shop → Potions & Protection → Veil Potion. Review the price, balance and money left or shortfall, then use the separate Confirm button to buy one stored potion. Opening the review, repeating the buy command, going Back or closing it does not buy anything. Buying or carrying it does not activate the veil; drink it first. You can also use /veil buy.

Every drink adds eight real-time minutes to whatever time remains. Two fresh drinks give about sixteen minutes. Use /veil status to check your remaining time. Time keeps running while you are offline and through ordinary server restarts.

Your body becomes invisible and standard mobs ignore you, even in armor. Other players can still see your armor and held items. Harvesting, planting, tilling, chopping, mining and air swings keep the effect. Passive Thorns retaliation also keeps it.

Attacking ends all accumulated time. Hitting an animal, mob, player or other entity, firing an offensive projectile, or supported damage attributed to you reveals you. Milk, death, expiry or another effect removing/replacing invisibility also ends it. Another ordinary invisibility effect can block drinking a Veil Potion; wait for it to end. Do not brew Veil Potions: brewing removes their custom properties.

This is not invulnerability. Existing projectiles, explosions, collisions, environmental hazards and area attacks can still hurt you. Bosses, EliteMobs custom scripts, custom AI and other plugins are outside the first-version targeting guarantee and may behave differently. This is separate from Death Protection.

/mobveil status shows your veil; /mobveil buy starts the same reviewed purchase.

Read the Veil Potion player instructions on GitHub

Death Protection: two ways to keep your items through one death

ExcelDeathProtection 0.1.3 is installed, October 13, 2026. The new one-item purchase review is intended to make your balance and exact shortfall easier to see. The remaining Java and Bedrock-phone review-window checks, real purchases and gameplay acceptance for 0.1.3 were waived by the server owner on October 13, 2026; they are recorded as unverified, not passed. Startup and automated tests do not prove the in-game experience. The earlier 0.1.2 phone check covered shop-entry appearance only.

Death Protection — $15,000: protection starts immediately after you confirm the purchase. You receive no item and have no potion to drink. This option gives no extra powers or potion effects. A bar reading Inventory protected: 1 death indicates basic protection.

+Death Protection Potion — $30,000: buy a potion to store and drink later. Buying it does not activate protection; drink it first. While premium protection is active, it also gives Conduit Power: underwater breathing, clearer underwater vision, and faster mining. The mining boost also works on land. Its blue shell icon can come from other sources too, so use /dp status to check your protection.

How to buy: open Spawn Shop → Potions & Protection and choose either option. A review window opens with quantity fixed at 1, the product, price, your available balance and the money you would have left. If you cannot afford it, it shows Need $… more and disables purchasing. Prices use in-game money.

Only Confirm buys: select the enabled Confirm button to purchase one. Your current price, funds, eligibility and inventory space (for the potion) are checked again. Opening the review, repeating a buy command, refreshing, going Back or closing it never buys anything. Back to Spawn Shop cancels the review and returns to the shop.

Refresh when needed: the balance preview updates about once per second, but a review expires after 20 seconds. If it has expired, the price changed or it needs a new quote after you gained enough money, tap the disabled button to refresh the review, then use a separate Confirm. Refreshing itself does not charge you. The exact-shortfall display and Back navigation still need real phone/Java checks.

When you die: you still die and respawn normally, but you keep your inventory once. Neither option saves your XP; the server's normal XP rules still apply. That death uses up your protection. Active protection survives logging out and ordinary server restarts.

Only one active protection at a time: if either kind is active, buying basic protection is blocked before you are charged. Drinking another protection potion is also blocked, and you keep the potion. An undrunk potion in your inventory is not active protection.

Already protected, or have an older potion? Existing active protection and old Death Protection Potions stay premium, with no extra payment. Check your active tier at any time with /dp status.

You can also check or buy in chat: /deathprotection status shows what you hold; /deathprotection buy starts the same reviewed purchase.

Read the Death Protection player instructions on GitHub

Quantity and price controls

ExcelShops 1.1 retains the in-place button update. Java price and quantity plus/minus buttons update the existing inventory instead of closing and reopening it. Java cursor checks are still open. For phone shopping, use the Bedrock buying walkthrough; for typed prices or quantities, see exact amounts below.

Type exact shop amounts

ExcelShops 1.1 is installed. The Java Enter Amount button lets you type a price or quantity in a private in-game text window. It does not send your text to public chat. Mike confirmed the Bedrock phone flow on October 14, 2026; Java display and input checks are still open.

  1. Set a price: open your own chest/sign → Edit item prices → choose an item → Enter Amount. Type 0.50 for fifty cents per item, then choose Use amount. New shop setup uses the same field. Review the shop, then separately confirm publication.
  2. Choose a quantity: On Java, use an item’s Enter Amount button, type a positive whole number such as 19, then choose Use amount. On Bedrock, the Spawn Shop uses item details → Enter exact amount; submitting returns to that same item’s details. Continue with the buying walkthrough above. Player shops count individual items; Spawn Shop counts bundles where labeled. Check the final item count and total.

Prices accept 0.01 through 100000000.00 with up to two decimal places, without a dollar sign or commas. For example, 70000 is a valid price, but not a valid item quantity. Quantities are bounded by stock, bundle size, inventory capacity and a maximum of 2,304 delivered items. Zero, negative and invalid numbers are refused. Use the dialog’s Cancel button to keep the previous amount; Escape is disabled in this Java entry window. Invalid numbers show an explanation and let you try again. Java text entry expires after 60 seconds, and an old response cannot change a newly opened screen.

Bedrock keeps its native number forms. For player shops and the standalone fallback catalog, shared inventory buttons remain available instead of a Java-only dialog when a form cannot open. The Bedrock category-shop route instead closes safely if its native forms are unavailable. Java plus/minus buttons keep the in-place window update, and Java falls back to them if its native dialog API fails. Older translated Java clients still need a display check.

Other device checks are still open: Java keyboard/mouse, other Bedrock phones, Bedrock console/controller and Bedrock Windows. Mike’s phone check does not verify every device or a live purchase.

Shopkeepers: villager shops

What it does: Shops run by villagers. Walk up, right-click (or tap) the villager, and trade, like buying at a market stand.

These trade stands are different from the green Spawn Shop villager, who opens the Spawn Shop menu instead of trading.

Server menu compass

Created by us: ExcelMenuJoin (MenuJoin) supplies the compass, and ExcelBedrockMenu (BedrockMenu) supplies the phone/console menu. These are documentation labels; installed names and commands are unchanged.

What it does: The compass in your bag opens the server menu. Use it (right-click or tap), then pick a button. Both menus include Spawn, the shop button (Excel Shop), Adventurers Guild, Party, Server Commands, Server Website, since October 19, 2026, a Utility Pouch button and, since October 25, 2026, a What's New button for the latest server news. On the phone and console menu the Utility Pouch button now sits fourth, right after Party (Spawn, Teleport to a player, Party, Utility Pouch), moved up in ExcelBedrockMenu 1.5.14 on October 21, 2026 because players reach for it so often, and the Server Commands list now includes /pouch. While the guild is sealed, tapping Adventurers Guild opens the Road to the Guild quest page (also at /seals) instead of travelling; once the last seal falls, that same button runs /ag to the guild hall again. Since ExcelBedrockMenu 1.5.8, the phone menu's teleport buttons (Spawn, Adventurers Guild while it is unlocked, and the /spawn, /home and /ag entries) ask "Teleport to …?" first; Cancel or closing the prompt does nothing. The separate Road to the Guild button was removed in ExcelBedrockMenu 1.5.6, and the phone menu now shows the locked guild button as plain "Adventurers Guild". The Java menu check of these changes is not yet confirmed. The phone/console menu also includes Leaderboard and Teleport to a player. Since October 20, 2026 (ExcelBedrockMenu 1.5.12), the phone/console menu also has a Skills & Perks button between mcMMO Stats and Leaderboard: tapping it opens the skill ladders in brief, the commands for checking your levels, and the Magic line with the live broken-seal count, and its Skill Trees on the Website button shows this website's address for the full ladders — so on a phone or console, /mcstats is no longer your only in-game way to look at your skills.

Since October 26, 2026 (ExcelBedrockMenu 1.5.22), the phone and console menu matches the computer compass: it adds My Cosmetics, Ask the Guide (type your question in a box and the Valley Guide answers in chat) and Maker's mark and Gear stats, and mcMMO Stats now lives inside Skills & Perks as My mcMMO stats, the same as on a computer.

Also since October 26, 2026: Server Commands has a group called Your Items & Help with /lostfound, /trash, /mark and /ask. On a computer it is row 2 of More Server Commands. On a phone or console it is the 8th group (ExcelBedrockMenu 1.5.23), and /mark and /ask work without typing. The compass on a computer also has a Wand controls button (the same as /wandstyle), matching the phone and console button.

Jobs & Money menu shortcut: removed from the Java menu at about 9:22 p.m. Central on October 12. The Bedrock menu still has that button; its removal is prepared but not installed. Jobs earnings, balances and the Spawn Shop’s balance display are unchanged. Update October 25, 2026: the tidier Java compass menu has a Jobs & Money button again, in its Me row.

Always with you: You always respawn with your Server Menu compass. It does not drop when you die, and you cannot throw it away.

On the phone/console menu, Teleport to a player: tap it, then tap a family member's name. They get a request and tap Accept (or Decline). Your bed sets where you respawn; there are no home teleports.

What you can type:

  • /menu opens the same menu
  • /seals shows the Road to the Guild quest page — which of the five seals are broken and what is left to break
  • /compass get a new Server Menu compass if yours is missing
  • /pouch opens your Utility Pouch (see below)
  • /trash (or /bin) opens the trash bin; /trash chest trashes from a chest; /trash undo brings your last trash back; /trash help explains it (see below)

If the menu opens when you join and you would rather it didn't, type /menujoin — it toggles the auto-open. The Server Commands menu has the same toggle as a button.

The Utility Pouch: a pocket for your server gear

The Utility Pouch (ExcelPouch 0.1.0, live October 19, 2026) is a small extra pocket that only holds server gear. Open it with /pouch, or tap the Utility Pouch button in the server menu: both the computer menu and the phone menu have the button now.

It has two pockets: one for your Server Menu compass, and one for your Magic wand, for when wands arrive at Seal 1: when yours comes, it goes in the wand pocket. Only your own bound wand fits the wand pocket, and nothing else goes in the pouch at all. It is not a backpack. The pouch keeps whatever you park in it until you take it out.

Parking your compass is easy: open the pouch, move the compass into the compass pocket, and close it — that is where your compass goes when you park it, and it stays there until you take it out. A parked compass stays out of your way: the server knows yours is parked, so it will not hand you a second one. Take it back out whenever you like. And what is parked stays parked through death: if you die with your compass parked, the pouch keeps it — die, respawn, and your pouch still holds exactly what you left in it.

The trash bin: throw things away, safely

The trash bin (ExcelTrash 0.3.0, updated October 26, 2026, about 2:07 AM Central) is where you throw away things you do not want. Open it with the Trash Bin button in the compass menu, or type /trash (or /bin). It works the same on computers, phones, tablets and consoles, and every button says what it does right on its name, so you never need to hover.

  1. Put the things you do not want in the trash slots (the top three rows).
  2. Tap OK once. Nothing is deleted yet: the button turns into Tap again to delete with the number of items, so you can check.
  3. Tap it again within 5 seconds to delete. If you wait, it goes back to normal and nothing is deleted.
  4. Chat says Deleted and how many items. Changed your mind? Tap Recover, or type /trash undo, within 5 minutes to get them all back (one time).

Pressed OK with nothing in the trash slots? It says Nothing to delete, and nothing happens. Closing the screen without OK, or tapping Cancel (the door), gives everything back, so nothing is lost by accident.

How do I trash things from a chest? You cannot have a chest and the trash bin open at the same time, so the trash bin opens the chest for you. Two ways:

  • Look at the chest first. Stand near the chest, look right at it, and open the trash bin. The first button reads Trash from the Chest you're looking at (or Barrel, or Shulker Box). Tap it. You can also look at the chest and type /trash chest.
  • Not looking at one? Tap the button that says Trash from a chest: tap, then tap the chest. The trash bin closes. Now tap (right-click) the chest, barrel or shulker box you want, within 30 seconds.

The chest opens inside the trash bin: the chest's own items are at the top and the trash slots are at the bottom. Tap a chest item to move it down to the trash slots, then tap OK twice, the same as above. /trash undo puts trashed chest items back in that chest. It only works on chests you are allowed to open: your own, or ones in a claim where you have been trusted. Empty the trash slots before you switch to a chest.

What if I die with the trash bin open? Nothing in the trash slots is deleted. Items from a chest go back to that chest. Your own items drop with the rest of your things, or, if you keep your inventory when you die, they stay in your inventory.

Need a reminder? The book button in the trash bin, or /trash help, explains it all in chat. The first time you open the trash bin, chat shows a short tip too.

How do I delete stuff? How do I trash items from a chest? How do I get trashed items back? Open the trash bin (compass Trash Bin, or /trash), put items in the top slots, and tap OK twice. For a chest, look at it and tap the chest button, or tap the chest button and then tap the chest. Get things back with Recover or /trash undo within 5 minutes.

Leaderboards: who's on top

Created by us: ExcelLeaderboard and ExcelWealthBoard. ExcelWealthBoard was called WealthBoard until October 16, 2026; it is a name change only.

ExcelWealthBoard 1.4.0 is installed (formerly WealthBoard 1.3.0-mobile.7; the popup looks and behaves exactly the same). When you join, the Wink Reyes Valley welcome popup appears once you are in the world, with the three richest players shown underneath it.

The three rankings take turns underneath the title, one at a time, a few seconds each, so every full name and balance fits on a phone screen instead of running off the edge. The layout is the same for Java and Bedrock, with no permanent sidebar and no shortened names.

What you can type:

  • /leaderboard opens the boards menu: Top Miner, Top Builder, Most Playtime, Top Slayer, Top Traveler, Most Deaths, Richest, mcMMO Power, Jobs Levels and Land Claimed. Land Claimed ranks everyone by how much land they have claimed, and every row also shows how many claim blocks that player can still claim. On a phone it is a button list; on Java it is a clickable chat menu. Mike confirmed the phone version works on October 15; the Java chat-menu check is still to come.
  • /baltop shows the wealth rankings
  • /mctop shows the mcMMO rankings

If there are no recorded balances yet, the popup says No balances yet - go earn!

No Phantoms

Created by us: ExcelNoPhantoms 1.2.0. It was called NoPhantoms until October 16, 2026; version 1.2.0 (October 18, 2026) runs the event.

Phantoms are flying monsters that show up if you skip sleeping for a few nights. Here they follow the Phantom Debt: while the Ender Dragon lives, they hunt on Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Thursday nights, and the guild's wards keep Friday, Saturday and Sunday safe. Sleeping is the shield, and when the dragon falls they are turned off for good. Read the full story.

Compact-label update installed; appearance accepted

ExcelPlayerMap 0.2.1 is installed on the family server. Startup, the safe map-data migration, and the existing update interval are confirmed. The update replaces oversized labels with compact five-pixel names and thin letter-shaped shadows, without solid label, arrow, or footer rectangles.

Appearance passed by owner report, October 12: the server owner personally tested the update and reported that the map looked perfect. The client platform was not supplied, so this confirms the reported visual result without claiming every Java/Bedrock device or gameplay case was tested.

Before this update, a player reported successful map creation, a self-nameplate that follows movement, and visible local terrain. A later screenshot also shows two player names/markers and terrain. The player's Java/Bedrock platform is not known, so this is not a crossplay pass. Off-map edge-arrow behavior still needs a player check. Sharing, zoom, locking, portals, and wider gameplay checks remain pending. The former BlueMap web map has been removed.

Use an ordinary empty map as usual. The upgrade adds player names and arrows to newly activated maps while you hold them, keeping Minecraft's normal terrain and exploration. No browser, resource pack, or client mod is needed by this design.

Start a mapRead the markersZoom, copy, and frameTroubleshooting

Start a map normally

  1. Get an ordinary empty map and use it to start mapping the area. No map-giving command is needed.
  2. On a phone, hold it in your main hand. Java players can also hold it in their offhand.
  3. Explore normally to fill in the terrain. The added names show eligible online players in the same world, including players beyond the map's terrain coverage.

Ordinary maps from before the original map-feature installation, explorer maps, and treasure maps are not automatically upgraded. Maps already enrolled with WRVPlayerMap keep tracking after the ExcelPlayerMap update. Custom maps are also skipped. The add-on does not give you free maps on join, death, or reconnect. Type /playermap for help.

What the names and arrows mean

  • North is up. Your name and marker are green, with a * beside your name. Other players' names are yellow.
  • Inside the mapped area, arrows show which way a player is facing.
  • At the edge, arrows point from the map's center toward an out-of-area player. This may be a different direction from your own current position.
  • Lines connect labels to their real positions. When players stand together, the labels spread out to help you read them.
  • On a crowded map, some labels take turns being shown. Type /playermap list for the full text list of visible online players and their worlds.

A map of one area

The map keeps its fixed, vanilla-aligned area. It does not follow or continuously recenter on you. Faraway unexplored terrain is not filled in automatically.

Use the normal cartography table

  • Add paper to zoom out. The five normal scales cover 128, 256, 512, 1,024, and 2,048 blocks across. A larger area means less detail.
  • Add an empty map to make a copy. You can share maps with family; they are not owner-locked.
  • Add a glass pane to make a locked copy. That locked map stays plain, without the added live-player overlay. Use locked maps for walls and map art.

Zoomed maps keep Minecraft's normal exploration behavior. A held map and an unlocked framed copy with the same map ID can share rendered imagery, so an unlocked frame is not a separate, reliable live-player display.

Changing dimensions: the map remains a map of its original world. It does not switch to a different map when you travel through a portal. Players in another world are not placed at converted coordinates.

If something looks wrong

  • No added names? Check that this is an unlocked, enrolled ordinary map and hold it in your hand. Previously enrolled WRVPlayerMap maps still qualify; older untracked and special maps keep their normal behavior.
  • A player is missing? They may be offline, in another world, or hidden from the added overlay by permissions or supported vanish settings. Use /playermap list to check. Minecraft's ordinary map cursors still follow its own rules.
  • The map is crowded? Labels cycle about every three seconds at the default update rate. Long or unsupported name characters may be shortened or shown as a question mark.
  • Lost a map? There is no automatic replacement. Keep a normal copy somewhere safe if you want one.

Player positions are sampled about once per second, with extra client/network delay possible. The basic player report does not verify phone/Java-specific readability, Geyser behavior, or live-server performance; those checks remain open.

Full Player Map instructions on GitHub · Installation checks · Map source review

ExcelBuildMode 1.3 pilot · installed October 16, 2026 (1.1 and 1.2 on October 14)

Builder Mode: build big without touching your Survival life

Created by us: ExcelBuildMode. Its in-session editing tools come from WorldEdit (third-party, by EngineHub), installed alongside it; without WorldEdit, build sessions would not have their selection, clipboard and undo tools.

Builder Mode is a construction-only mode for trusted builders. While it is on, you build in Creative with flight. The moment you enter, your Survival life is snapshotted: inventory, armor, offhand, ender chest, XP and hotbar. When you switch back, everything is restored exactly as it was.

Using itNo earning while buildingWorldEdit toolsSafety and restorePilot status

Who can use it, and how

  • Marvin is the only builder in the pilot, by the server owner's decision. More builders can be added only by the owner.
  • Type /buildmode on to enter a build session, /buildmode off to return to normal play, and /buildmode status to check which mode you are in.
  • Everyone else plays completely normally. Builder Mode changes nothing for players who never enter it.

No earning while building, by design

  • Jobs pays nothing for anything you do during a build session.
  • Combat is off both ways: you cannot hurt anything, and nothing can hurt you.
  • Projectiles, fishing, harvesting and stripping are blocked (composters, sweet berry bushes, beehives, campfires, and axe-stripping logs).
  • You cannot talk to NPCs or open shops during a session.
  • Rewards you already earned in normal play before entering still settle normally.

WorldEdit inside a session

  • Selections, set, replace, walls and faces work, along with clipboard copy, paste, rotate and flip, and undo/redo of your own edits.
  • Added in 1.3: //cyl, //hcyl, //sphere, //hsphere, //stack and //schem (save, load and list only). Marvin reports Build Mode and //sphere working in game (October 16); the full command list he tried was not detailed, and //schem end to end and Bedrock use are unconfirmed.
  • Server-wide limits apply to everyone: at most 5,000 blocks per operation, a maximum radius of 50 for shapes (raised from 16 to 21, then to 50, on October 16), a 10-step undo history, and every WorldEdit command is logged.
  • Brushes included (1.2): WorldEdit brush commands (//brush) work in session: the construction brush families, plus the brush options for size, material, range and mask. The butcher brush is deliberately not included; it kills mobs in a radius, which is combat, not construction.

Safety and restore

  • You cannot be hurt during a build session, so there is no dying in Builder Mode.
  • If you log out mid-flight, you rejoin in a protected landing hold (still flying, empty hands), and your Survival items return the moment you land.
  • Restore also runs on normal exit and on the recovery paths after a rejoin or a server restart, so a session cannot strand your Survival inventory.

Pilot status

Live acceptance run passed, October 14, 2026

ExcelBuildMode 1.1 was installed on October 14, 2026, in the same restart as ExcelShops 1.1 and WorldEdit 7.4.5. Marvin ran the live acceptance checks the same evening and they passed: building earned nothing, harvesting and stripping were blocked, the bow and fishing rod were blocked, NPCs and shops were blocked, WorldEdit edits and selections worked, and leaving Builder Mode restored his items and XP exactly. That run found one gap: WorldEdit brushes were not in the 1.1 permission grant.

ExcelBuildMode 1.2 was installed later the same evening, in the same restart as the EliteMobs one-currency switch, closing the brush gap. Live startup and the loaded 1.2 version were verified. ExcelBuildMode 1.3 was installed October 16, 2026 at 7:57 PM Central (more WorldEdit commands, see above). Marvin reported it working in game that evening. Still unchecked: a Bedrock (phone, iPad or tablet) Build Mode check, Marvin's live brush check in a session, watching a queued Survival payout settle during a session, and party behavior (shared XP and credit) while a builder is in a session.

BlueMap web map removed

The BlueMap browser map was removed on October 12, 2026. There is no longer a live BlueMap web map to open. Your Minecraft worlds and player progress are unchanged by this removal.

The held-map upgrade is installed. See the Player Map guide for instructions and the remaining player checks.

Behind the scenes

These work quietly in the background. You never need to touch them.

  • Geyser, Floodgate, ViaVersion, ViaBackwards: Let phones, iPads, tablets, consoles, and computers all play together, even on different game versions.
  • LuckPerms: Decides who is allowed to do what.
  • Vault: Keeps track of everyone's money so the other add-ons agree on it.
  • PlaceholderAPI and CMILib: Helper pieces the other add-ons need.
  • Chunky: Builds the world ahead of time so it loads faster.

Skill Trees

Every perk in the valley, on one page: what it does, the exact level it unlocks on this server, and how strong it is here. mcMMO skills are live right now. Magic is active since Seal 1 broke on October 23, 2026 (1 of 5 seals): the Healer's school is open now, and its tree below shows what opens as each further seal falls.

Open the Skill Trees (mcMMO and Magic)

Live now · mcMMO

The mcMMO Skill Tree

Nineteen skills and eighty-three perks, exactly as this server runs them. Levels run from 1 to 1,000 here (mcMMO Retro Mode), so most chances grow a little with every level instead of jumping in big steps. Tap a skill to walk its ladder.

Check your own skills in game

  • /mcstats shows all of your skill levels on one page.
  • /mining opens one skill's own page. Type any skill's name (like /smelting or /repair) to see your exact current chances for its perks.
  • /mctop shows the server leaderboards.

Unlocks announce themselves. The moment you cross a perk's level, the game posts a clickable "[ mcMMO @ Skill Rank Unlocked!]" message in chat, with a sound, so you hear about new perks as you earn them. Level-ups show in the action bar and chat too.

Perks marked From the start have no unlock level at all: they work from your first day and quietly grow stronger as you level. That is why a bonus like Smelting's Second Smelt can feel like it appeared from nowhere. It was always on.

Good to know

  • Bonuses stack: Friday through Sunday, mcMMO and vanilla XP earn at 1.2x (ExcelWeekendXP), and playing near your party shares mcMMO XP (ExcelTogether).
  • Jobs pays cash for many of the same activities, but Jobs has no level perks of its own. Its only boost is the Jobs weekend bonus: 1.2x XP and money for all jobs, all day Friday, Saturday and Sunday (Friday added October 23, 2026).
  • Player-vs-player fighting is off on this server. The live server setting was checked on October 26, 2026. Player-only mcMMO perks (Daze, Disarm, Iron Grip and Limit Breaks) are described below for reference, not as available player fights. Limit Breaks do not add damage against mobs. Party or claim membership does not turn PvP on. This corrects the guide, not the gameplay settings.
  • Salvage is a child skill: it grows from your Repair and Fishing levels together.
AcrobaticsRoll away fall damage, dodge attacks, and move like you mean it.3 perks · all on from the start

Gain XP by: surviving falls that hurt you, and dodging in combat. (Our ExcelAcrobatics plugin tunes how Acrobatics XP is earned; it does not change these perks.)

From the start Roll

When fall damage hits, a chance to roll and negate it: 0.1% per level, reaching 100% at level 1000. A roll protects up to 7 damage (3 and a half hearts). Turned on in coreskills.yml.

From the start Graceful Roll

Sneak while falling to double your Roll chance, and a graceful roll protects up to 14 damage (7 hearts).

Level 1 Dodge

In combat, a chance to dodge and take half damage: 0.02% per level, reaching 20% at level 1000. Dodged damage is divided by 2.

AlchemyBrew faster, stretch your fuel, and unlock special potions.2 perks · all on from the start

Gain XP by: brewing potions.

From the start Catalysis

Brewing stands work faster as you level: speed ramps evenly from 1x at level 0 to 4x at level 1000.

From the start Concoctions

Special brewing ingredients unlock by rank. Rank 1 (level 0): the vanilla potion set. Rank 2 (100): Carrot for Potion of Haste, Slimeball for Potion of Dullness. Rank 3 (200): Quartz for Potion of Absorption. Rank 4 (350): Apple for Potion of Health Boost, Rotten Flesh for Potion of Hunger. Rank 5 (500): Brown Mushroom for Potion of Nausea, Ink Sac for Potion of Blindness. Rank 6 (750): Fern for Potion of Saturation. Rank 7 (900): Poisonous Potato for Potion of Decay. Rank 8 (1000): Golden Apple for Potion of Resistance.

ArcheryArrows that hit harder, daze targets, and come back to you.4 perks · all on from the start

Gain XP by: shooting mobs with a bow.

From the start Daze

Arrows have a chance to daze a player target, forcing their view up and adding 4 damage (2 hearts): 0.05% per level, reaching 50% at level 1000. Player targets only, never mobs. PvP is off on this server, so this is a reference mechanic, not an available player-combat perk. No rank gate in skillranks.yml; it scales from the start.

Level 1 Skill Shot

Arrows hit harder: +10% damage per rank, with ranks at levels 1, 100, 150, 200 and every 50 after, up to +200% at rank 20 (level 1000). The bonus damage itself is capped at 9 (4 and a half hearts).

Level 1 Arrow Retrieval

When you kill something with your bow, a chance to get your arrows back: 0.1% per level, reaching 100% at level 1000.

Level 100 Archery Limit Break

Extra damage against very high-health targets, one rank every 100 levels to 1000. Player targets only (Limit Break PvE disabled), never mobs. PvP is off on this server, so this is a reference mechanic, not an available damage bonus.

AxesChop through armor and land the big critical hits.6 perks · first unlock at level 1

Gain XP by: hitting mobs with an axe.

Level 1 Critical Strikes

A chance for a critical hit: 0.0375% per level, reaching 37.5% at level 1000. Crits deal double damage to mobs and 1.5x damage to players.

Level 1 Armor Impact

On a hit, a flat 25% chance to damage the target's armor. The durability damage grows with your rank (DamagePerRank 6.5, ranks at 1, 100, 150, 200 and every 50 after to 1000).

Level 50 Axe Mastery

Axes simply hit harder: +1 damage at level 50, +2 at 100, +3 at 150, +4 at 200.

Level 50 Skull Splitter

Active ability: right-click with an axe, then your hits splash half damage (DamageModifier 2.0) onto nearby enemies.

Level 100 Axes Limit Break

Extra damage against very high-health targets, one rank every 100 levels to 1000. Player targets only (Limit Break PvE disabled), never mobs. PvP is off on this server, so this is a reference mechanic, not an available damage bonus.

Level 250 Greater Impact

A flat 25% chance on a hit for a huge knockback (1.5x velocity, like Knockback II) plus 2 bonus damage.

CrossbowsTrick shots and powered shots with a crossbow.3 perks · first unlock at level 1

Gain XP by: shooting mobs with a crossbow.

Level 1 Powered Shot

Bolts hit harder: +10% damage per rank, with ranks at levels 1, 100, 150, 200 and every 50 after, up to +200% at rank 20 (level 1000). The bonus damage itself is capped at 9 (4 and a half hearts).

Level 50 Trick Shot

Bolts ricochet into extra nearby targets. Rank 1 at level 50, rank 2 at 200, rank 3 at 400; the rank is how many bounces you get, and each bounce hits softer than the last.

Level 100 Crossbows Limit Break

Extra damage against very high-health targets, one rank every 100 levels to 1000. Player targets only (Limit Break PvE disabled), never mobs. PvP is off on this server, so this is a reference mechanic, not an available damage bonus.

ExcavationDig up treasure, flint, and double drops from dirt and gravel.2 perks · first unlock at level 1

Gain XP by: digging dirt, sand, gravel, clay and similar blocks with a shovel.

Level 1 Archaeology

Digging can unearth treasures, and better treasures unlock by rank at levels 1, 250, 350, 500, 650, 750, 850 and 1000. Each digging material (dirt, gravel, sand and so on) has its own treasure list.

Level 50 Giga Drill Breaker

Active ability: right-click with a shovel, then dig with instant breaks and triple the treasure chance while it lasts.

FishingBetter treasure, enchanted catches, and food from your catches.6 perks · first unlock at level 1

Gain XP by: catching fish and treasure.

Level 1 Treasure Hunter

Fishing can pull up treasure, and better treasure tiers unlock by rank at levels 1, 250, 350, 500, 650, 750, 850 and 1000. Your rank also multiplies the vanilla XP you earn from fishing: x1, x2, x3, x3, x4, x4, x5, x5 across the eight ranks.

Level 1 Master Angler

Fish bite sooner. Every rank trims the wait (10 to 30 ticks per rank from advanced.yml, with a bigger trim when you fish from a boat). Ranks at levels 1, 200, 300, 400, 600, 700, 800 and 900.

Level 50 Ice Fishing

Cast into a frozen lake and the ability punches a small hole in the ice so you can fish there.

Level 150 Shake

Hook a mob with your rod to shake its drops loose, even skulls. Chance by rank: 15% at 150, 20% at 200, 25% at 250, 35% at 300, 45% at 400, 55% at 500, 65% at 600, 75% at 700.

Level 200 Magic Hunter

A chance to fish up enchanted items and gear. The chance itself is set in the fishing treasures config, not the numbers files used here.

Level 200 Fisherman's Diet

Fish you eat restore more hunger: +1 per rank, with ranks at levels 200, 400, 600, 800 and 1000.

HerbalismDouble harvests, instant replanting, and green-thumb luck.7 perks · all on from the start

Gain XP by: harvesting crops and plants.

From the start Hylian Luck

Breaking grass and flowers with a sword can turn up rare items: 0.01% per level, reaching 10% at level 1000. No rank gate in skillranks.yml; it scales from the start.

From the start Shroom Thumb

A chance to spread mycelium onto dirt and grass: 0.05% per level, reaching 50% at level 1000. No rank gate in skillranks.yml; it scales from the start.

Level 1 Double Drops

A chance for double drops when you harvest: 0.1% per level, reaching 100% at level 1000.

Level 50 Green Terra

Active ability: right-click with a hoe for a chance at triple harvest drops while it lasts, and use seeds on blocks to turn them mossy or planted.

Level 200 Farmer's Diet

Plant foods (bread, cookies, melons, carrots, potatoes and more) restore more hunger: +1 per rank, with ranks at levels 200, 400, 600, 800 and 1000.

Level 250 Green Thumb

Supported crops can replant even before level 250, starting at the earliest growth stage. Chance grows with level, reaching 100% at level 1000. Ranks at 250, 500, 750 and 1000 improve the replanted growth stage, not the list of supported crops. Keep the matching seeds or crop item; sneaking skips replanting.

Level 1000 Verdant Bounty

The level-cap perk: a chance at triple drops on every harvest. It scales over a 10000-level curve in the config, so at level 1000 the live chance is 5% (ChanceMax 50).

MacesCrushing blows and a heavy density breach with a mace.3 perks · first unlock at level 50

Gain XP by: hitting mobs with a mace.

Level 50 Cripple

Mace hits can slow the target. Chance by rank: 10% at level 50, 15% at 200, 20% at 400, 33% at 800.

Level 100 Crush

Maces hit harder: +1.5 damage at level 100, then +1 more per rank at levels 250, 750 and 900, reaching +4.5 before armor.

Level 100 Maces Limit Break

Extra damage against very high-health targets, one rank every 100 levels to 1000. Player targets only (Limit Break PvE disabled), never mobs. PvP is off on this server, so this is a reference mechanic, not an available damage bonus.

MiningDouble drops, tougher tools, and blast mining.6 perks · first unlock at level 1

Gain XP by: mining stone and ores with a pickaxe.

Level 1 Double Drops

A chance for double drops from every block you mine: 0.1% per level, reaching 100% at level 1000. It even works alongside Silk Touch (SilkTouch: true in advanced.yml).

Level 50 Super Breaker

Active ability: right-click with a pickaxe, then mine with instant breaks and a triple-drop chance while it lasts (AllowTripleDrops: true).

Level 100 Blast Mining

Crouch and right-click TNT from up to 100 blocks away to detonate it remotely. Ranks at levels 100, 250, 350, 500, 650, 750, 850 and 1000 raise the bonus ores from blasted blocks from 35% up to 70%, grow the blast radius (+1 up to +4), and from rank 4 cut the blast damage you take (25%, 25%, 50%, 50%, then 100% at rank 8).

Level 100 Bigger Bombs

The blast-radius half of Blast Mining: your TNT explosions reach further at every rank. Unlocks with Blast Mining rank 1.

Level 100 Demolitions Expertise

The safety half of Blast Mining: explosions hurt you less at every rank, down to no blast damage at all at rank 8. Unlocks with Blast Mining rank 1.

Level 1000 Mother Lode

The level-cap perk: a chance at triple drops from mining. It scales over a 10000-level curve in the config, so at level 1000 the live chance is 5% (ChanceMax 50).

RepairRepair gear before it breaks and keep its enchantments.3 perks · first unlock at level 1

Gain XP by: repairing gear on the mcMMO anvil, which is an iron block here (not a vanilla anvil).

Level 1 Repair Mastery

Every repair restores more durability: a bonus of 0.2% per level, reaching +200% at level 1000.

Level 100 Arcane Forging

Repairs can keep an item's enchantments. Keep chance by rank at levels 100, 250, 350, 500, 650, 750, 850 and 1000: 10, 20, 30, 40, 50, 50, 60, 60 percent. If an enchant is not kept cleanly it may be downgraded instead (chance falls from 75% at rank 1 to 10% at rank 8) or lost, and kept enchants are capped at level 5.

Level 400 Super Repair

A chance that a repair works at double effectiveness: 0.1% per level, reaching 100% at level 1000.

SalvageBreak down old gear and fishing catches into useful materials.2 perks · first unlock at level 1

Gain XP by:

Level 1 Scrap Collector

Salvage tools and armor back into their materials. Higher ranks, at levels 1, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300, 350 and 400, return a bigger share and can salvage more heavily damaged items; at high ranks you always get at least 1 material back.

Level 100 Arcane Salvage

Salvaging an enchanted item can pull its enchantments out as books. Across ranks at levels 100, 250, 350, 500, 650, 750, 850 and 1000, the chance to extract a full enchant climbs 2.5% to 32.5%, and a partial (weaker) enchant 2% to 17.5%. Enchants may downgrade (capped at level 5) or be lost.

SmeltingSecond ingots from the furnace, longer fuel, and more smelting XP.3 perks · first ranked unlock at level 100

Gain XP by: smelting ores and materials in furnaces.

Every smelt has a chance to produce one bonus item on top of the normal result: 0.05% per level, reaching 50% at level 1000. This is the perk behind the "extra iron from nowhere" reports; it scales from the very start, with no rank gate.

Level 100 Fuel Efficiency

Your fuel burns longer in the furnace: burn time x2 at level 100, x3 at 500, x4 at 750.

Level 100 Understanding the Art

The vanilla XP you earn from smelting is multiplied by your rank at levels 100, 250, 350, 500, 650, 750, 850 and 1000: x1, x2, x3, x3, x4, x4, x5, x5.

SwordsBleeding hits, counters, and serrated strikes.5 perks · first unlock at level 1

Gain XP by: dealing damage with a sword.

Level 1 Rupture

Your hits can open a bleed: 5 seconds of pure damage that armor cannot soften. Chance by rank: 15% at level 1, 33% at 150, 40% at 750, 66% at 900. Top-rank bleed ticks are 1.0 damage against mobs and 0.3 against players.

Level 50 Serrated Strikes

Active ability: right-click with a sword, then your hits splash a quarter damage (DamageModifier 4.0) onto nearby enemies and can apply Rupture.

Level 100 Swords Limit Break

Extra damage against very high-health targets, one rank every 100 levels to 1000. Player targets only (Limit Break PvE disabled), never mobs. PvP is off on this server, so this is a reference mechanic, not an available damage bonus.

Level 200 Counter Attack

While blocking, a chance to reflect half the damage back at your attacker: 0.03% per level, reaching 30% at level 1000. Reflected damage is the incoming damage divided by 2.

Level 750 Stab

A late-game passive that adds +2.5 damage at level 750 and +4 at level 1000 before armor. The calculation is base 1.0 plus rank times 1.5, not base times a multiplier.

TamingA wolf partner that grows stronger and fights at your side.10 perks · first unlock at level 1

Gain XP by: taming wolves and ocelots, and fighting alongside your wolves.

Level 1 Beast Lore

Left-click a wolf or ocelot to inspect its stats.

Level 1 Call of the Wild

Sneak and left-click holding 10 bones to summon a wolf, 10 cod for an ocelot, or 10 apples for a horse. Summons last 240 seconds; per-player limits are 2 wolves, 1 ocelot and 1 horse at once. A summoned horse's jump strength lands between 0.7 and 2.0.

Level 100 Environmentally Aware

Your wolves teleport to safety away from cactus and lava hazards, and take no fall damage.

Level 150 Gore

Your wolf's attack gains another copy of its base hit damage before other bonuses and armor. This is extra hit damage, not a bleed effect; a leftover chance setting does not establish a chance roll in the checked implementation.

Level 200 Fast Food Service

When your wolf lands a hit, a flat 50% chance it heals itself.

Level 200 Pummel

Your wolves' hits have a flat 10% chance to pummel the target with extra force.

Level 250 Thick Fur

Your wolves take half damage (divided by 2) and resist fire.

Level 350 Holy Hound

Adds health when your wolf takes magic, poison or wither damage, which can help offset the hit. The original damage can still apply: this is not immunity or guaranteed net healing.

Level 500 Shock Proof

Explosion damage to your wolves is divided by 6.

Level 750 Sharpened Claws

Your wolves deal +2 damage.

Leaves break instantly when you hit them with an axe.

Level 300 Knock on Wood

While Tree Feller runs, felled logs can drop bonus finds such as apples. Two ranks: level 300 and level 600.

Level 1000 Clean Cuts

The level-cap perk: a chance at triple drops from logs. It scales over a 10000-level curve in the config, so at level 1000 the live chance is 5% (ChanceMax 50).

Active since Seal 1 · 1 of 5 seals broken

The Magic Skill Tree

Magic is active since Seal 1: the seal broke on October 23, 2026, at about 5:46 PM Central, and the Healer's school opened (stage 1). Since October 24, 2026, about 2:05 AM Central, stage 1 has 43 spells in the world, including the healer's kit. Get your first wand from the Old Quartermaster. The Adventurers Guild itself stays locked, and as the family's remaining seals fall, magic seeps back into the valley one school at a time. The guild is at 1 of 5 seals today.

How magic leveling works

  • One shared SP balance upgrades your wand. Successful eligible casts earn Magic spell points (SP). You can focus on one spell, but its casts contribute to your wand's shared SP, not a separate per-spell XP meter. Your normal green Minecraft XP bar and mcMMO levels are separate.
  • The same wand ranks up automatically: Beginner → Student costs 800 SP; Student → Apprentice costs 1,600 SP; Apprentice → Master costs 1,800 SP. Each upgrade spends that step's cost and keeps any surplus SP. You do not buy a replacement wand. Your mana pool grows from 60 to 100, 150 and 200.
  • Ability rank is not independent proficiency. Abilities use the levels available at your wand's rank. Casting one ability repeatedly does not level it ahead of the wand, and not every wand upgrade raises every ability. Seals separately decide which spells are available; they do not cap your wand rank.
  • See both kinds of progress: select or hold your wand, then open Server Menu → Spell Book or use /spellbook. The header shows your current wand rank and SP toward the next rank, for example "Student: 340/1,600 SP to Apprentice" (an example, not your balance). Open an ability's details to see its current Rank and, where available, the next applicable wand tier and what its effects change to. There is no separate grindable ability-XP progress bar.
  • Earning pace: the same spell's SP earning recharges proportionally over 60 seconds, rather than having a hard once-per-minute payout lock. Healing spells must really heal someone. Across all spells, each player earns full-rate SP through 800 earned in a Central-time day, then 25% rate until 1,600 earned that day, then no more until the next day. Switching spells does not reset that shared daily limit.
  • If the gain message flashes away: use Spell Book to check your SP. The short message above the hotbar can be replaced by stance or other status text; a disappearing message does not prove that progress was lost. This documentation does not announce a fix for that display issue.
  • Server XP bonuses count for SP too: the weekend bonus (Friday to Sunday) gives SP x1.2, and casting with a party member within 50 blocks gives SP x1.25. Together they make x1.5, the most there is. When a bonus is on, your Spell Book says so (for example "Bonus now: SP x1.25 (group)").
  • Magic is built on the Magic plugin by NathanWolf (elMakers), configured by us for this server, with one spell of our own: Storm Shackle.
  • Magic went in dormant on purpose, with no player trial first (the owner's call on October 17, 2026), and stayed asleep until Seal 1 broke on October 23: the family becomes the test as each school opens. The seals decide when each school opens.

Combat Attunement

Active since Seal 1. ExcelAttunement went onto the server on October 20, 2026, ahead of the first wand, and switched on when Seal 1 broke on October 23. Now that the Old Quartermaster hands out wands, the rule below governs mixing weapons and spells.

Combat Attunement. Fighting commits you. Land a hit with a weapon — sword, axe, mace, trident, bow or crossbow — and for the next 30 seconds your wand goes quiet: no casting until the stance fades. Cast a fighting spell instead (or heal while you're in the middle of a fight) and your weapons go quiet for 30 seconds the same way. Healing or buffing BEFORE a fight is fine — that's just good preparation, and utility spells like Recall never lock you at all. Your stance and its countdown sit in the action bar above your hotbar, so you always know which side you're on and how long is left.

Stance lock (ExcelAttunement 0.1.5, live since October 24, 2026; scroll fix 0.1.7, October 26, 2026). After a combat spell you are in caster stance: your weapons stay locked for 30 seconds, and the bar above your hotbar counts down. After a weapon hit you are in fighter stance: your wand waits instead. Taking hits keeps your stance going. Since version 0.1.7 the scroll wheel (and controller bumpers) skips over the locked item, so every other hotbar slot, potions included, is always reachable; only the locked slot itself refuses.

Magic is off in the EliteMobs worlds. Magic is silent inside the Adventurers' Guild and the EliteMobs dungeons. Your wand stays a plain item there, and short Magic buffs fade at the door. Spells that would carry you into those worlds, like Recall, are turned aside. The guild's own classes and gear do the fighting. Magic works as before everywhere else in the valley.

The five seals

Each seal falls when the family earns any one of these victories, in any order: the Ender Dragon, the Wither, the Elder Guardians of an ocean monument, the Warden, or a raid victory. The first seal opens the first school below, the second seal the second school, and so on.

Every school of magic is open after this seal. The Masons' building spells go to every mage on the Master band. Every block they conjure is temporary (gone again in 4 to 60 seconds) and respects land claims. The spells that change the world for everyone (Sunny Day, A Dark and Stormy Night, Weather and Time) open here too, each with a long wait between casts so nobody can flip the sky all evening.

Opens with the family's fifth and final seal. The Adventurers Guild itself opens with this one.

Your magic is already complete: every school opened with the fourth seal. The fifth seal opens the Adventurers Guild instead: /ag to the guild hall and its own world of adventures.

Staying locked for good: lava, explosions, grenades, flamethrowers, nuke-class spells, Wither summons, shop spells, and vengeance. They do not fit a family server, and no seal opens them. When the fourth seal falls, the full tree is open: 104 spells on the Master Wand. When the fifth seal falls, the Adventurers Guild itself opens.

All plugins

Every add-on currently installed on the server. The server runs on Paper 26.3 (build 214), by the PaperMC team.

Show all 62 plugins

Which plugins are ours?

Look for Created by us for our custom add-ons, or Modified by us where we changed an existing plugin. Our website and GitHub use an Excel name (original name) so you can recognize both. Other plugins keep their authors’ names and credit.

These are documentation labels. The wider runtime rename has been canceled. Installed plugin names, commands, permissions and data folders stay as they are. The eight custom add-ons with original names in parentheses still use those names in-game. ExcelPlayerMap and ExcelShops already use their Excel names; WRVPlayerMap and WRVShops identify their original projects.

Excel XP (mcMMO) identifies our Together/green-bar XP and indicator changes to upstream mcMMO; it is not a claim that we created mcMMO. Jobs has customized settings, which are labeled separately from plugin-code changes.

Not installed: ExcelProgression is our beginner-menu prototype. The separate Jobs XP code modification is still a draft; live Jobs has only the documented settings changes. The modified ChestShop compatibility plugin was removed. See the prototype status and GitHub source repository. Original source-folder names are kept for compatibility.

Version 0.2.1 teaches the Guide to think. Questions with no scripted answer now get a composed answer drawn from this website's knowledge; questions about you are answered with your real numbers from the server itself; and good answers are remembered, so the next time the same question is asked the Guide answers instantly, from what it learned. Type /ask followed by your question, start a chat message with “Guide,”, or ask about the server in ordinary chat and the Guide may answer: joining, the seals, shops, claims, skills, and your own stats. When it does not know, it says so instead of guessing, and unanswered questions are logged so its knowledge can grow. Type /guide off if you do not want it listening. ExcelTelemetryCreated by us0.1.0installed October 19, 2026, about 7:24 AM CentralNEW, and entirely behind the scenes: the server's flight recorder. Like a flight recorder, it quietly writes down every kill (who landed the damage, with what weapon, and how the damage was shared), every player death (the cause, and the gear the player was wearing), every boss fight from start to finish — the wins and the party wipes alike — and every mcMMO level-up. In short, it tracks kills, deaths, boss fights and level-ups, and nothing else. Nothing changes in game and there are no player commands; you will never see it, and that is the point. The records are private: they are kept off the shared drives, and they exist so the five seals can be tuned fairly — how fast bosses fall, who carries a fight, and whether the road is too hard or too easy. Mike reads a pull of the records each week, alongside the Valley Guide report. 81 of 81 automated checks pass.
