A party is the group that earns the bonus: when 2 or more party members are within 50 blocks of each other, Jobs money and XP are multiplied by 1.25 (+25%). Strangers standing nearby no longer count (0.1.0 counted anyone nearby; a setting can bring that back). It stacks by multiplying with the Jobs weekend bonus, so a +20% weekend plus the party bonus is 1.5x, not 1.45x. Invite from the menu (Invite to party), by right-clicking a player while holding the compass (it asks Yes or Cancel first), or with /excelparty invite NAME. The other player gets a 60-second Accept or Decline popup like /tpa. Up to 5 per party, any member can invite, and My party shows who is in it with a Leave button. It also sends a matching mcMMO party invite, so accepting joins both; if mcMMO says no, the ExcelTogether party still works. Version 0.2.1 adds shared party XP: every party member within 50 blocks gets 100% of the Jobs money and XP and mcMMO skill XP another member earns, times 1.25, on top of other boosts such as weekend XP. It also mirrors hand joins, quits and kicks between mcMMO parties and ExcelTogether. The mcMMO party-bonus range was raised from 32 to 50 blocks to match. Built and tested. Sharing is for the same job only, members idle for 2 minutes get no shared gains, and mcMMO XP is not shared twice when a party leader turns on mcMMO /party xpshare. Parties made only in ExcelTogether are not saved over a restart (mcMMO parties are rebuilt at login). Mike saw the "XP bonus +1.25 | Together" bar in game on October 16 (about 8:38 PM Central). UNCONFIRMED with real players: Java and Bedrock checks, the Jobs money bonus, shared party XP and a real invite through mcMMO. Version 0.3.0 adds the party range indicator: while you are in a party, the action bar above your hotbar shows your party range state, refreshed every couple of seconds. Green means the bonus is paying: "Party bonus ACTIVE: N in range (x1.25)", where N is how many party members are within 50 blocks of you. Dim gray means "Party: nobody in range". When you come into range, a "Party bonus ON" title flashes on screen with a soft sound; when you drift out of range it flashes "Party bonus OFF" (no sound). The flashes wait about 3 seconds before switching, so standing right at the edge of the range does not make them strobe. The indicator uses the exact same 50-block test as the bonus itself, so what it shows is what you are paid for. Turn the flashes and sound on or off with /excelparty cue off (or on); the choice lasts until you log out. Still UNCONFIRMED with real players: the two-player indicator check on Java and on Bedrock. Source and tests: open draft record on GitHub.

A safe moment on Bedrock: on phones, tablets and consoles you cannot be hurt for 4 seconds after you join, respawn or change worlds, so the loading screen never costs you a life (the void, /kill and lava still count). Night Hearing, from Seal 2: once the second seal falls, at night in the overworld, nearby zombies hear loud noises inside a base (doors, breaking or placing blocks, fighting, shooting, chests and note blocks) and come for whoever made the noise. Sneaking halves how far a noise carries, and footsteps stay silent. A zombie that hears you still has to get through your doors the usual way, and it gives up after a minute if it gets nowhere. Night Hearing is waiting for now, while 1 of 5 seals is broken. Tested on our test server; not yet checked on a real phone. Every zombie can now break doors, on a tier ladder — wooden doors fall in about 14 seconds, copper doors fall to a ~45-second siege, iron doors last about 60 seconds before they fall — so better doors buy more time to get out and fight. Historical WandBars versions and their original test notes

These records describe earlier releases, not current control guarantees. Use the current controls above where they differ.

Version 0.3.0 with 0.2.9 (installed October 26, 2026, about 5:09 PM Central): your own spell bar and an organized Spell Book. Each player picks up to 21 spells for the bar (3 pages of 7); every other spell the wand knows waits in the Spell Book, sorted into 8 groups (Healing, Defense, Damage, Movement, Summon, Building, World, Utility), with an A to Z list and gray locked spells that show which rank opens them. Change the bar from the Spell Book, from a spell's page, or with /spellbar. Same on Java and Bedrock. Version 0.2.8 (installed October 26, 2026, about 12:13 PM Central): wands rank up when you cast. Every successful spell with your wand earns spell points (SP); at 800 SP the wand becomes a Student wand by itself, then 1,600 SP to Apprentice and 1,800 SP to Master. Same wand, every spell kept, new ones learned, and the whole server sees a short cheer in chat. Each spell pays about once a minute, healing spells pay only when they heal, a daily limit keeps the pace even, and the weekend (x1.2) and party (x1.25) bonuses count, up to x1.5. Your SP is at the top of your Spell Book. Version 0.2.7 (installed October 26, 2026, about 11:47 AM Central): in Tap bar and Tap to pick your wand moves to the last hotbar slot (slot 9) by itself, and whatever was there swaps into the wand's old slot; nothing is dropped or deleted, and it waits while your bar or any screen is open. In Tap bar on a phone, tablet or console, tap the wand with the bar up to get your items back, and tap again to open it (computers: Q or right-click, unchanged). Spell info now shows real numbers from the server's spell settings (hearts healed, damage, effects, duration, radius, cost, cooldown, range) and what the next rank changes. Still to check in game: tapping the wand on a real phone. See the change log. Version 0.2.6 (installed October 26, 2026, about 9:48 AM Central): Tap to pick really picks. Tapping a spell icon now only picks that spell and never casts it, also for the healing spells that used to go off on a tap (Cure, Boon, Feed and others). On a phone, tablet or console, press and hold the screen to cast the spell you picked; it casts once and the bar stays up (before, the hold could close the bar). On a computer, the hotbar keys pick, left-click casts, and right-click or Q puts your items back (right-click again brings the bar back). Tap bar, crouch paging and the other styles did not change. Still to check in game: a real phone and a real computer. See the change log. Version 0.2.5 (installed October 26, 2026, about 3:10 AM Central): the Tap bar puts every spell you have on your hotbar, 7 to a page, with one empty slot kept for getting your items back; crouch flips pages only when they do not all fit. On a phone, press and hold the screen to read the spell list or the spell you just tapped (it never casts and the bar stays up). Sneak bars can now be picked on a computer too, Tap to cycle is now called Tap to pick, every style says what crouch does, and the Tap bar and compass no longer flicker when you scroll during a stance. Still to check in game: hold-to-read and crouch paging on a real phone, and the same on a real computer. Known issue (fixed in 0.2.6): in Tap to pick, tapping an icon could cast it instead of picking it. This was already the case in 0.2.4. Version 0.2.4 (installed October 25, 2026, about 2:30 AM Central): /wandstyle now has five styles: Classic, Sneak bars, Cycle, Tap to cycle and Tap bar (best on a phone). On a phone, the spell list is a Spell Book page that shows your rank. Everyone still starts on Classic. Version 0.2.1 (installed October 24, 2026, about 6:42 PM Central): the second style is now called Sneak bars and is for phones and consoles (right-click opens the bar, a sneak press flips to the next bar). Computer players always use Classic. A short wand tip shows until you press OK. NEW. Your wand controls, your choice. Everyone starts on the Classic controls (see How to use your wand). Type /wandstyle to switch to Spell bars and back; on a phone or console it opens a two-button screen. Your choice is saved and comes back after a rejoin, a respawn, a new wand or a new seal. Still to check in game: the switch on a real phone.
ExcelWhatsNewCreated by us0.1.1installed October 26, 2026, about 9:38 AM Central; 0.1.0 on October 25, 2026, about 10:41 AM CentralVersion 0.1.1 (installed October 26, 2026, about 9:38 AM Central): news pages now go both ways. When you are reading an older entry, a Newer news button takes you one step back toward the newest, next to the Older news button. On a computer they are clickable [Newer news] and [Older news] buttons at the end of the entry; on a phone, tablet or console they are buttons in the news window (OK when the entry is still waiting for your OK, Newer news, Older news, and Close when there is no OK button). On a phone, /whatsnew list now names the real range you can type (for example "/whatsnew 2 to /whatsnew 9") instead of always saying "2 (or 3)". Version 0.1.0: New. Server news inside the game. Type /whatsnew (also /news), or tap What's New on the compass menu, to read the latest news; /whatsnew list shows older entries (up to the 10 newest since October 26, 2026; it was 3 before). When a new entry is added, everyone gets a short popup once, a few seconds after joining, at a calm moment: on phones and consoles it is a small window with an OK button, and on a computer it is a chat message with a clickable [OK]. Press OK and that entry won't show again. Each entry lists new commands, commands that got better, and other changes, in plain words.

AutoSortThird-party, by nothub0.1.17installed October 24, 2026, about 6:42 PM CentralNEW. Type /autosort (or /sort) to turn sorting on or off just for you. When it is on, your chests, barrels, shulker boxes and ender chest tidy themselves. It is off until you turn it on.
Excel Projectile Assist (ExcelAimAssist)Created by us0.1.3installed October 21, 2026, about 10:14 AM CentralShots from bows and crossbows, and thrown tridents, bend gently toward a monster you were already aiming at, so near misses connect. And if the monster is moving, the shot leads it — it aims at where the monster will be, not where it was. The bend aims at the monster's chest, and the shot's arc is allowed for, so the drop over distance does not pull it low. It covers weapon projectiles only — snowballs, eggs, ender pearls, and potions fly exactly as thrown. It never locks on by itself: the monster has to already be inside a cone around your shot (25 degrees, out to a reach of 60 blocks, with a clear line of sight), and if nothing is in the cone the shot flies exactly as released, however far it goes. It is invisible in game — no messages, particles, or sounds — and works the same on computers (Java) and on phones, tablets, and consoles (Bedrock). Turn the aim assist off any time with /aimassist off (and back on with /aimassist on); your choice is remembered across restarts. 178 of 178 automated checks pass. Still to check in game: a real near-miss bend on a computer and on a phone.
ExcelTreeCleanupCreated by us0.1.0installed October 20, 2026, about 6:39 AM CentralTidy treetops: if you chop most of a tree and the last couple of logs are left floating up in the leaves, the valley tidies them away — the leftover logs come down as drops and the leaves fall on their own. It only ever touches a small floating remnant (up to 3 logs) of a tree you were just chopping: anything still standing on the ground, part of a build, or inside someone's claim is never touched, and normal chopping is never changed — this is not a tree feller. Turn it off for yourself any time with /treecleanup off (and back on with /treecleanup on); your choice is remembered across restarts. Works the same on computers (Java) and on phones, tablets, and consoles (Bedrock). 84 of 84 automated checks pass, plus a full test-server rig. Still to check in game: one real chop leaving a treetop, on a computer and on a phone.

Back to top

Close to vanilla

Difficulty: Hard in the Overworld, Nether, and End. The valley moved from Normal to Hard on October 19, 2026 (set and read back in all three worlds that afternoon). The owner made Hard permanent on October 25; the trial and its verdict check are closed. This is the vanilla difficulty setting; EliteMobs keeps its separate settings.

No starter kits. Everybody starts fresh. Keep inventory is off, so take care of your things when you explore. If you want a safety net, the paid exceptions are the two Death Protection options.

Family protections. This is a family server, and the rough edges stay quiet for its youngest players: they get a gentler time of it, entirely in the background, with nothing visible changing for them. The nights still belong to everyone who fights them — every zombie can now break doors: a wooden door falls in about 14 seconds, a copper door lasts through a ~45-second siege, an iron door lasts about 60 seconds before it falls. Better doors buy more time to get out and fight.

Tidy treetops. If you chop most of a tree and the last couple of logs are left floating up in the leaves, the valley tidies them away — the leftover logs come down as drops and the leaves fall on their own. Anything still standing on the ground, part of a build, or inside someone's claim is never touched, and you can turn it off for yourself any time with /treecleanup off.

Rules

  1. Be kind to everyone.
  2. No griefing or stealing.
  3. Ask before building near someone else.
  4. No cheats or x-ray.
  5. Have fun!

The server went to owner-only (whitelist on, nobody removed) at about 4:16 PM; the last player online left on their own at about 5:03 PM, two checks at 5:07 PM showed nobody online, and after a reason, a one-minute warning and a 10-second countdown in chat it saved and shut down at about 5:08 PM, started right away, was fully up at about 5:09 PM (19.9 seconds) and opened to everyone again at about 5:10 PM. What changed (ExcelWandBars 0.2.9 and 0.3.0): Your own spell bar. You choose up to 21 spells for the bar (3 pages of 7 in Tap bar, or 8 + 8 + 5 in Sneak bars); every other spell your wand knows stays in the Spell Book and is never lost. Your bar starts as your wand's first 21 spells, so today nothing moves: even a Master wand at Seal 1 knows 17 spells and all of them stay on the bar. When your wand learns a spell (by ranking up or when a seal breaks), it goes into the first empty slot, and chat says "New spell: X added to your bar", or that it is in your Spell Book when the bar is full. Change your bar from the Spell Book ("My spell bar (change it)", then tap a slot to move it, take it off or pick a spell), from a spell's page ("Put on my bar", "Move on my bar", "Take off my bar"), or with /spellbar (also /mybar): /spellbar slot 4, /spellbar put heal 3, /spellbar remove 3, /spellbar swap 1 2. The organized Spell Book (/spellbook or the Spell Book button): your wand, how many spells are on your bar and in the book, your rank and SP, then one button per group: Healing, Defense, Damage, Movement, Summon, Building, World and Utility, plus "All spells A to Z". Each group lists spells from the lowest rank up; spells on your bar show their slot. Spells a higher rank of your wand will learn show gray with the rank that unlocks them (for example "unlocks at Apprentice"), and only for seals that are already broken, so nothing is spoiled. Commands: /spellbook group healing, /spellbook all. Java and Bedrock: the same screens on both: Bedrock gets forms (12 spells a page with Next and Back buttons), Java gets chat pages of 10 with clickable Prev, Groups and Next.   • The Valley Guide now stays quiet when you talk to another player by their nickname or a short form of their name. Installed live in one short restart on October 26, 2026, with nobody online. The owner approved it at about 1:10 PM Central, after it passed our test server. The server was closed to players (whitelist on, owner only, nobody removed) at about 1:43 PM, and four checks between about 1:43 and 1:44 PM all showed 0 players. Why: since October 25 the Guide answers game questions asked in ordinary chat if nobody else answers within about 45 seconds. It is meant to stay out of lines where you are talking to another player, but it only knew a line was aimed at someone when you typed their whole name as one word. So a line like "hey short name, is your map broken?" looked like a question for the Guide, and after 45 seconds it could post a public answer nobody asked for. A check of real unanswered chat found 2 lines like that. What changed: the Guide now also counts these as talking to a player: the parts of a player's name (a name like "QuietOtter42" counts as "quiet" or "otter"), neighbouring parts together, the first 4 or more letters of a one-word name, and chat nicknames set with /nick (colours, tags and the leading "~" are ignored). A name followed by "s" (like "otters") counts too. It works the same on Java and Bedrock, and the dot at the start of Bedrock names is ignored. To avoid false matches, a part never counts on its own if it is shorter than 3 letters, all numbers, a common word (like cool, king, pro, star, dragon), or a game word the Guide answers about (like fox, creeper, village). What you notice: chat to another player by nickname or short name gets no Guide answer, just like typing their full name always did. Questions that do not name a player are answered exactly as before: website knowledge first, then AI as a last resort, a public answer after about 45 seconds if nobody else answers, player answers remembered when thanked. /ask and starting a line with "Guide," still always answer. One tradeoff: if a line uses the name or short name of a player who is online (or who chatted since the last restart), the Guide treats it as talking to them, even if it is also a question, for example "yo name, what do you think?". If you want the Guide's answer, use /ask or start with "Guide,". Tested: 55 of 55 new name checks passed (made-up names only), every older Guide test gave the same result as before, and a replay of 128 real unanswered chat lines showed only the expected changes (3 fewer lines sent to the AI: the 2 nickname lines and 1 line that named a player). On our test server, a short name line and a nickname line got no answer and no AI call after 55 seconds, a plain question got its public answer, and /ask answered. It also starts cleanly if the nickname plugin is missing. After the install: the Guide started with the same knowledge as before (174 chunks), AI fallback on, delayed chat answers on (45 seconds), no errors from the Guide. No settings changed. Still to check in game: a nickname or short name line gets no Guide answer after 45 seconds, and a plain question (or /ask how do I tame a fox) still gets an answer. Rollback: the previous version (0.3.3) is kept as a backup and can be put back with one restart. Plugin: ExcelGuide 0.3.3 to 0.3.4 (created by us). Record: wrv-plugins draft PR #89.

  • Wands now rank up when you cast. Installed live in one short restart on October 26, 2026, with nobody online. The owner approved it at about 12:07 PM Central. The server was closed to players (whitelist on, owner only, nobody removed) at about 12:10 PM, and two checks about two minutes apart (about 12:10 and 12:12 PM) both showed 0 players. After a reason, a one-minute warning and a 10-second countdown in chat it shut down at about 12:13 PM, started right away, was fully up at about 12:14 PM (19.2 seconds), and opened to everyone again at about 12:17 PM. Nobody was kicked. Why: the wands could never rank up: Magic's rank wands came with spell point earning turned off, so casting paid nothing. The owner asked that wands rank up by being used, at a pace of about two days of normal play per rank, that the server's XP bonuses count too, and that the whole server gets to cheer each rank-up. How it works: every successful spell you cast with your wand pays spell points (SP). At 800 SP your wand becomes a Student wand by itself, then 1,600 SP to Apprentice and 1,800 SP to Master. It is the same wand, still bound to you, every spell you had stays, and it learns the new rank's spells at once. You get a title and a sound, and everyone online sees a short line like "name reached Student rank!". Each spell pays its full SP about once a minute, and Heal, Mend, Flash Heal and Greater Heal pay only when they really heal someone, so mixing spells is the fastest way. A daily limit (full pay up to 800 SP a day, then a quarter) keeps the pace even. XP bonuses count: the weekend bonus (Friday to Sunday) gives SP x1.2, casting with a party member within 50 blocks gives SP x1.25, and together x1.5, the most there is. Where to see it: the top of your Spell Book shows your rank and progress (for example "Student: 340/1,600 SP to Apprentice") and any bonus on now, and a short line above your hotbar shows each gain, like "+2 SP | Beginner 2/800". It waits a moment if the party line is showing, so the two never overlap. Everyone starts at 0 SP; nothing was taken away from anyone. Seals still decide which spells exist; they never cap your rank. ExcelWandBars 0.2.8.
  • Your wand moves to the last hotbar slot, tap the wand to get your items back, spell info shows real numbers, and a blue mana bar shows your mana as a number. Installed live in one short restart on October 26, 2026, with nobody online. The owner approved it at about 11:40 AM Central. Two checks a minute and a half apart (about 11:42 and 11:44 AM) both showed 0 players. The server was closed to players (whitelist on, owner only, nobody removed) at about 11:43 AM, and after a reason, a one-minute warning and a 10-second countdown in chat it shut down at about 11:47 AM, started right away, was fully up at about 11:48 AM (19.6 seconds), and opened to everyone again at about 11:50 AM. Nobody was kicked. The mana bar went in after that with a Magic reload at about 11:53 AM, no second restart. Why: the owner asked for three things: in Tap bar the wand should sit in the last slot by itself, tapping the wand should give you your normal items back, and the spell info screen should be detailed and accurate (real numbers, like how much a heal heals) and say how to rank up. He also asked that players can see their mana as a number. 1. The wand moves to slot 9 (Tap bar and Tap to pick): the first time you hold or pick your wand in one of those two styles, it swaps into the last hotbar slot (slot 9). Whatever was in slot 9 swaps into the wand's old slot at the same moment. Nothing is ever dropped, deleted or copied. It waits while your spell bar is up, while any other screen is open, while you have an item on your cursor, for 3 seconds after you click in your inventory, join or respawn, while you are dead, in spectator or creative, and while you are holding slot 9 yourself (it moves as soon as you look away). A wand in your main inventory (not the hotbar) is left alone. Picking the wand moves it, selects slot 9 and opens your bar as usual, without casting anything. Classic, Cycle and Sneak bars never move the wand. 2. Tap the wand for your items (Tap bar): on a phone, tablet or console, with the spell bar up, tap the wand again: the bar closes and your items come back exactly as they were. Tap it again to open the bar. On a computer nothing changed: Q or right-click puts your items back, the same as before. The /wandstyle button on a phone now reads "Tap bar (tap wand: items back)" and "Tap: cast. Hold: read. Crouch: page", and on a computer "Tap bar (Q: items back)". The wand tip says the same. 3. Spell info shows what a spell really does: press and hold to read a spell (or /spellinfo) and you now see real numbers taken from the server's own spell settings: hearts healed, damage, effects and their levels, how long it lasts, who it hits, the radius, cast time, what stops it, mana cost, the real cooldown and range, and what the next rank changes. Examples: Mend on a beginner wand "Heals 0.75 heart" (1 heart at the next rank), Heal "0.5 heart every 1 sec for 10 sec, 5 hearts in all", Feed "Heals 2 hearts, cures Poison and Wither, fills 3 drumsticks". Where the next rank is not open yet it says so: "Student rank is not open yet. Watch What's New." 4. A blue mana bar with a number: while you hold your wand, a thin blue bar at the top of your screen shows "Mana" and your mana as a whole number, like "Mana 42", and its fill shows how full you are. It counts up as your mana refills. It does not use the line above your hotbar, so it never covers your spell, stance, range or page lines. Java and Bedrock see the same bar. It also stays when the next seal breaks. What did not change: your spells, wands, ranks, mana amounts, refill speed, costs and cooldowns. The Tap bar still puts every spell you have on your hotbar, 7 to a page, and crouch still flips pages. The stance scrolling fix, Classic, Cycle, Sneak bars and Tap to pick controls are the same. Tested: on our test server, 1,050 automatic checks passed, including 2,000 random hotbars where every item was kept exactly once. A computer-style test player passed 23 of 23 checks (wand to slot 9, waiting while holding slot 9, picking the wand, tapping the wand to close and reopen the bar, inventory clicks, cursor items, relog, death and respawn, creative, main inventory), with exact item counts every time and nothing on the ground. The mana bar was tested there too. After the install the server showed the new spell info ("Heals 0.75 heart" for Mend) and the phone tap hook switched on. Still to check in game: tapping the wand on a real phone (our phone-style test player could not send that tap, so it is not tested yet; if it does not work, the empty slot and press and hold still give your items back), the wand moving to slot 9 on a real phone and a real computer, and the mana bar on both. Also: a new What's New entry, "Tap bar: your wand lives in slot 9", tells everyone about it once on their next join. Plugin: ExcelWandBars 0.2.6 to 0.2.7 (created by us; it sets up Magic wands, by elMakers). The mana bar is a Magic (elMakers) wand setting, the same Magic version. Nothing in EliteMobs (by MagmaGuy) changed. Record: wrv-plugins draft PR #86.

After the start the server came up cleanly in about 19 seconds with all 71 plugins, no serious errors and only the usual known start-up notices, and the seal count was unchanged.   • Wand spell switching fixed, and Mend now costs real mana. Installed live in one restart on October 26, 2026: after a one-minute warning and a countdown with nobody online, the server shut down at about 6:10 AM Central, started at 6:14 AM, was fully up at about 6:15 AM, and opened to players again at about 6:17 AM. 1. Spell switching fix (Magic, by elMakers): on a wand set to step through its spells, switching to the next spell (right-click or tap, F, or drop) could silently do nothing. When the wand's current spell was not found before an empty slot in the wand's spell list (for example after a seal changed your spells, or after you moved a spell in the picker), Magic stopped with an error instead of skipping the empty slot. On October 25 this showed up 60 times in 18 minutes in the server log. We added one safety check so empty slots are skipped. Nothing else in the plugin changed: every other file is the original, and the version still reads 11.2.5. What you notice: wands that used to get stuck now move on to the next spell, the same on a computer and on a phone, tablet or console. No controls, spells, mana or settings changed in this fix. On a test server, beginner, student and master wands whose current spell was missing all failed before the fix and all moved to the next spell after it. 2. Mend now costs real mana: Mend is the healer's quick filler heal with no cooldown of its own. It was so cheap that spamming it almost never emptied the mana bar: a beginner could cast it about 60 times in a row and a student about 100 times, which made every other heal pointless. Mend now costs about twice as much, tuned so nonstop Mend empties a full bar in about 20 casts (about 20 seconds) at every wand level. Beginner wand (60 mana): heals 1.5 health (three quarters of a heart), was 2 mana, now 4. Student (100 mana): heals 2 (1 heart), was 4, now 8. Apprentice (150 mana): heals 2.5, was 7, now 12. Master (200 mana): heals 3 (1.5 hearts), was 12, now 17. At higher seals the shared cooldown is a little shorter, so the bar empties in about 16 to 18 casts. Boon tip: Boon stops mana from refilling while it is on, so nonstop Mend with Boon on empties the bar in about 11 to 15 casts. Turn Boon off when you heal. Refill: once you stop healing, mana refills in about 15 seconds from empty on a beginner wand and about 10 seconds on the others; the first 5 seconds after your last heal refill slowly, then full speed. What did not change: how much Mend heals, its range and its 1-second shared cooldown; every other spell (Flash Heal, Heal, Ward, Greater Heal, Cure, Boon); wand mana pools and refill speeds. Java and Bedrock: exactly the same numbers and the same mana bar; tapping Mend costs the same as clicking it, and the spell info (press and hold on the Tap bar) reads the new cost from the plugin automatically. The change is in the spell settings for the current stage and in all five seal stage files, so it stays when the next seal breaks. Numbers: on a test server, cost per cast was exactly 4, 8, 12 and 17; nonstop casting emptied the bar after 20, 20, 22 and 20 casts (beginner, student, apprentice, master, at a slightly slower pace than the cooldown); refill took 15, 10, 9 and 9 seconds. After the restart the server started cleanly with all 71 plugins, 43 spells, no serious errors and no spell switching errors; the only new start-up message is a harmless notice that an EliteMobs update is available. Rollback: the original Magic plugin and every changed spell file are kept on the server as dated backups; putting the plugin back takes one restart, and the Mend costs can go back without one. Still to check in game: switching spells on a real computer and a real phone, and Mend spam on a student wand on both, watching the bar empty in about 20 casts. GitHub record: none; Magic is a third-party plugin and its settings are not in our plugin repository, so this change log and our records are the record.

The menus, the menu click stats and What's New were reloaded in the same start. The server then went down again for the night at 2:17 AM and reopens at 5 AM. The Valley Guide uses your controls (ExcelGuide 0.3.2 to 0.3.3). When an answer only uses mouse words (right-click, left-click, sneak), players on a phone, tablet or console also get one grey line explaining the touch and controller version (on a controller, right-click is LT and left-click is RT, and sneak is the crouch button). Computer players get a matching line when an answer only says tap. Answers that already give both ways get no extra line. Public answers pick the right line for each reader, and the AI is told to give both ways when it describes a control. 4. Trial vaults on phones and consoles (ExcelVaults 0.1.0 to 0.1.1). Tapping a used vault now says "Sneak + tap it, or type /vaults, to reopen it now for $X." and opens the Reopen / Cancel window right away, because phones cannot press chat buttons. Nothing is charged until you tap Reopen, and the window opens at most once every 30 seconds for the same vault. /vaults help explains it with tap words (on a controller: sneak, then press LT). On a computer the [Reopen now: $X] chat button works as before, and the /vaults list now says "Click a [Reopen] button". 5. What's New hints for both. 12 news lines that only told one side what to do now tell both. For example /trash gets "No typing: tap Trash Bin on the compass.", /autosort and /wandstyle get "Or click ... in the compass menu.", and /tips ok gets "Or click [OK] on the tip in chat." What you notice: on a computer, a new Wand controls button on the compass, a new row on More Server Commands, the vault list says Click, and What's New tells you which button to click. On a phone, tablet or console, a new Server Commands group, /cosmetics in Shops & Trading, Guide answers that explain the controls, a vault window that opens with a tap, and What's New tells you what to tap.   • The trash bin asks before it deletes, trashing from a chest is easier, and the phone and console compass gets three more buttons. Installed live at about 2:07 AM Central on October 26, 2026 (files in by 2:02 AM, the server started about 2:07 AM), in one restart with nobody online, during the overnight window when the server is locked to the owner. The server then went down again for the night at 2:08 AM and reopens at 5 AM. Why: the owner found that the trash bin deleted with no confirmation, that you cannot have a chest and the trash bin open at the same time, and that the chest feature was not explained anywhere. An overnight audit also found that items in the trash slots could be lost on death. Trash bin (ExcelTrash 0.2.0 to 0.3.0). OK asks first: the first tap turns the button into Tap again to delete with the number of items, and nothing is gone yet; tap again within 5 seconds to delete, or wait and it goes back to normal. If the number of items changes between taps, it asks again with the new number. After a delete, chat says how many items went, with a sound and a line above your hotbar, and reminds you that Recover or /trash undo brings them back within 5 minutes. With nothing in the trash slots, OK says Nothing to delete. This is the same on phones and consoles, where chat hides while a screen is open, because the answer shows on the button itself. Trash from a chest: look at a chest, barrel or shulker box (within 5 blocks) and open the trash bin, and the first button names it (Trash from the Chest you're looking at). Not looking at one? Tap the button, the trash bin closes, a title says Tap a chest, then tap the chest you want within 30 seconds. /trash chest does the same. Only chests you are allowed to open: claims, locks and shop chests are checked exactly as before. Help: clearer button names (the red side is now called the trash slots, and Cancel is an oak door, because the old barrier looked like the empty padding on Bedrock), a help book button, /trash help, and a one-time tip the first time you open it. Fixed: if you died with the trash bin open and kept your inventory, the items in the trash slots could be lost. Now they stay with you, and chat says nothing was deleted; if your bag is full, they come back when you respawn. On a normal death they drop with your other things, once. Chest items still go back to their chest. Unchanged: /trash, /bin, /trash undo, Recover (5 minutes, one time), the compass Trash Bin button, settings and permissions. Phone and console compass (ExcelBedrockMenu 1.5.21 to 1.5.22): it now matches the computer compass. New My Cosmetics button (after Excel Shop) opens your cosmetics. New Ask the Guide button (after What's New) opens a box: type your question, tap Send, and the Valley Guide answers in chat, the same as typing /ask. The menu never logs your question, and a typed slash command is only asked about, never run. New Maker's mark and Gear stats button (between Auto-sort and Auto-open on join) opens the two switches, the same as the computer settings. mcMMO Stats moved into Skills & Perks as My mcMMO stats, the same place as on a computer. The Skills & Perks Magic line was wrong: it still said Magic was sealed. It now says Magic is awake, shows how many seals are broken, and points to the Old Quartermaster in the spawn village for a first wand. The menu has 22 buttons (was 20); nothing else moved. Quiet fixes: Magic had 14 built-in recipes (brooms, a magic sword, goggles and others) for items this server does not use. They could never be crafted, and every start-up logged 28 warnings about them. They are now switched off; nothing you can craft changed, and wands still come from the Old Quartermaster and the seals. The rotating chat tip about /seals still said we were at 0 of 5 seals; it now says /seals shows the road and how many seals are already broken. Tips and news: a new trash tip explains the two taps and the chest button (the old trash tip is switched off), a short tip tells chest users the trash bin can empty a chest (shown at most twice, chat only), and What's New has the entry "Trash asks before it deletes". Java and Bedrock: every trash button says what it does on its name, so nothing needs a hover. On Bedrock the trash bin shows as a double chest, and the empty slots under it are padding. Numbers: trash bin 24 of 24 automatic checks and 41 of 41 test-server checks (both chest ways, two-tap OK, undo, help, and deaths with and without keep inventory); phone menu 224 automatic checks and 24 of 24 test-server checks with a test Bedrock player; the Magic recipe change tested on a test server (28 warnings to 0). After the install the server started cleanly with all 71 plugins and 0 errors. Start-up warnings went from 35 to 3 (the 28 recipe warnings are gone), What's New and the tips both checked with no warnings, and the guild is still LOCKED at 1 of 5 seals. Rollback: the old plugins are kept on the server as backups (ExcelTrash 0.2.0 and ExcelBedrockMenu 1.5.21), and every changed settings file has a dated backup; putting them back takes one restart. Still to check in game: the two-tap OK and both chest ways on Java and on a real Bedrock device, and the three new compass buttons on a real phone.   • Stance lock no longer blocks your hotbar (ExcelAttunement 0.1.7). Installed live at about 1:27 AM Central on October 26, 2026 (start clicked 1:26:45 AM, started about a minute later), in one restart with nobody online, during the overnight window when the server is locked to the owner. The server then went down for the night at 1:30 AM and reopens at 5 AM. What changed and why: Fighter and Caster stance still keep the other tool out of your hand: after a weapon hit you are in Fighter stance for 30 seconds and your wand cannot come into your hand; after a combat spell you are in Caster stance and your weapons cannot. Until now the lock did that by refusing to let your hotbar stop on the locked item, which also meant you could not scroll PAST it. A wand in the middle of your bar walled off every slot behind it, potions included, until the stance ran out, and a player died on Java reaching for healing potions this way. Now the lock steps aside. Java: the mouse wheel jumps straight over the locked item to the next slot. Example: wand in slot 5 in Fighter stance, scrolling right from slot 4 lands on slot 6, scrolling left from 6 lands on 4, and the bar still wraps from 9 back to 1. Number keys work for every slot except the locked one; pressing the locked slot's key does nothing, as before. The action bar still shows the stance and countdown (for example "Fighter stance: magic locked 18s"). Bedrock: tapping works as before, every slot except the locked one. On a controller, the bumpers (LB/RB) skip over the locked item the same way the Java wheel does. What did not change: the locked item never ends up in your hand, spells are still blocked in Fighter stance, weapon hits still do no damage in Caster stance, and the stance still lasts 30 seconds. No new player commands; admins: /attunement status now also counts "scroll hops". Numbers: 0.1.6 to 0.1.7. 977 automatic checks passed and 20 of 20 test-server checks (wand in slot 5, potions in slots 1 and 9, both stances, scrolling both ways and number keys); the same test on the old version got stuck at the wand. After the install the server started cleanly with all 71 plugins, the stance lock loaded, and the scroll-hop counter read 0. Rollback: the old plugin is kept on the server as ExcelAttunement-0.1.6.jar.bak-20261026-stance; putting it back takes one restart. Still to check in game: a Java player hits a mob with a sword (Fighter stance), then scrolls past the wand to a potion; and a Bedrock controller player using the bumpers (not yet tried on a real Bedrock device). GitHub record: wrv-plugins PR #79.

  • Harder monsters now step up automatically as each seal breaks (ExcelEliteLadder 0.1.0, new). Behind the scenes, no command for players. The plugin checks the seal count, and when a seal breaks it waits for a moment when nobody is online, then moves the wild elites to that seal's step: how many rise, how hard they hit and how hard your hits land. It only ever steps up, never down, saves a dated backup before each change, and refuses to change anything if the settings are not what it expects. The owner can check it with /eliteladder status. 57 automated checks passed. Today it holds the Seal 1 step; the next step, at Seal 2, is about 8 of every 100 spawns, about 90% harder, your hits at about 50%. (Updated to 0.1.1 on October 26 for the Seal 5 spawn rate.)   • A safe moment on Bedrock, and Night Hearing after Seal 2 (ExcelTrainingWheels 0.1.7). Bedrock safe moment (live now): on phones, tablets and consoles you cannot be hurt for 4 seconds after joining, respawning or changing worlds, while the loading screen clears. Falling into the void, /kill and lava still count. Java players are not affected. Night Hearing (waiting for Seal 2): once the second seal falls, zombies, husks and zombie villagers near you at night (overworld only, not the guild worlds) hear loud noises within 16 blocks: opening doors, trapdoors and gates, breaking or placing blocks, hitting, shooting, opening chests and other containers, and note blocks or jukeboxes. Footsteps are silent, and sneaking halves the range to 8 blocks. Up to 3 zombies come per noise, and no more than 6 at once for any one player, with a 20-second pause between noises. They head for the nearest closed door within 12 blocks and give up after 60 seconds. A wooden door holds about 12 seconds, a copper door about 45 seconds and an iron door about 60 seconds. Beds always work while Night Hearing is live, even with monsters nearby, and sleeping makes you silent. A tip, "The dead can hear you at night. Sneak, or sleep.", shows up to 3 times. Elite monsters and monsters already chasing someone ignore it. The same on Java and Bedrock. 497 automated checks passed. Still to check in game: the safe moment on a real phone, and Night Hearing after Seal 2. Record: open draft record on GitHub.
  • New: trial vaults reopen (ExcelVaults 0.1.0). A normal trial vault you already used reopens for you after 24 hours. Ominous vaults join in after the second seal and reopen after 72 hours. Click or tap a used vault to see how long is left. Reopen early: sneak and right-click the vault (or type /vaults, also /trialvaults, to list yours) and pay with your Jobs money: up to $750 for a normal vault (at least $50) and up to $3,000 for an ominous one (at least $200), less the closer it is to ready, rounded to $5. Paid ominous reopens are limited to 2 a day. On a computer you confirm on a screen or with a clickable chat button; on a phone or console you tap Yes on a pop-up. You still need a trial key, and each vault reopens only for you. There is also a tip and a What's New note. Still to check in game: a paid reopen on Java and on a real phone. Record: open draft record on GitHub.
  • The Guide answers plain-chat questions (ExcelGuide 0.3.2, was 0.2.3). Ask a game question in ordinary chat. The Guide looks it up and waits about 45 seconds. If a player answers first, the Guide stays out of it. If nobody answers, the Guide answers in public chat so everyone learns. If a player answers and the asker thanks them, the Guide remembers that answer and reuses it for the next person who asks, as "A player said: ..." with no name. Say "nvm" or "found it" to cancel. Private details, like your own money or claim numbers, never go in a public answer; ask those with /ask. Limits keep chat calm: one waiting question per player and four at a time server-wide. The same on Java and Bedrock. Its knowledge was refreshed from this website too. Still to check in game: a real plain-chat answer with players online. Record: open draft record on GitHub.

  • New: Build Flight Pass, fly inside your own claim. Installed October 25, 2026, about 11:18 PM Central. In the Spawn Shop, under Travel & Animals, a Build Flight Pass costs $1,000 and gives 30 minutes of flight, only inside your own claim. Double-tap jump to fly. Leave your claim or get in a fight (a mob hit, an arrow, or attacking something) and flight turns off and you land with no fall damage; the 30 minutes keep counting, and after the fight you can double-tap jump again if you are still in your claim. Buying again adds 30 minutes, up to 2 hours. /buildfly shows your time left. Right after you buy, the game explains how it works, and there is a new tip and a What's New entry. Your time keeps counting while you are offline. You get a warning at 5 minutes and at 1 minute left. Flight works only in a claim you own (not one you are trusted in) and never in the End. Commands: /buildfly (also /claimfly) shows your time left, /buildfly help explains it, and /buildfly buy opens the purchase. Java and Bedrock: double-tap jump on both; on a computer the purchase review is a chest screen, on a phone or console it is a pop-up form. If the pass cannot be turned on, your money comes back.   • Installed live at 5:03 PM Central: parties (WRV-063): ExcelTogether 0.2.0 plus ExcelBedrockMenu 1.5.7. UNTESTED with real players. You can now make a party and invite family. A compass click asks "Invite NAME to your party?" with Yes and Cancel before anything is sent. The other player gets a 60-second Accept or Decline popup, like /tpa. Up to 5 per party, and any member can invite. The +25% Jobs bonus now counts only party members who are nearby, so you need to party up first. The invite is also sent as an mcMMO party invite, and accepting joins both; if mcMMO refuses, the ExcelTogether party still works. If someone types /party accept by hand, that only joins mcMMO, not ours. Other commands: /excelparty (also /eparty and /together), /excelparty leave.   • Compass update (MenuJoin 1.2.0): you always get your menu compass back when you respawn, it no longer drops when you die, and you can't throw it away. Lost it anyway? Type /compass.
  • Menu update (BedrockMenu 1.4.1): Home buttons removed, Leaderboard button is back, and the teleport menu stays.
  • Home teleports turned off. Beds still set where you respawn, and Spawn and teleport requests still work.
